

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\App.js ====================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { useFonts, Poppins_400Regular, Poppins_500Medium, Poppins_600SemiBold, Poppins_700Bold } from '@expo-google-fonts/poppins';
import { Provider } from './src/store';
import { S } from './src/screens';
import { C } from './src/theme';
import { TabBar } from './src/ui';

const TABS = ['Home', 'Explore', 'Orders', 'Profile'];
const BAR = [...TABS, 'Cart', 'Checkout'];
export default function App() {
  const [ok] = useFonts({ Poppins_400Regular, Poppins_500Medium, Poppins_600SemiBold, Poppins_700Bold });
  const [stack, setStack] = useState([{ n: 'Splash' }]);
  if (!ok) return null;
  const cur = stack[stack.length - 1];
  const nav = {
    go: (n, p = {}) => setStack(s => [...s, { n, p }]),
    back: () => setStack(s => (s.length > 1 ? s.slice(0, -1) : s)),
    reset: (n, p = {}) => setStack([{ n, p }]),
  };
  const Scr = S[cur.n];
  return (
    <Provider>
      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: C.bg }}>
        <StatusBar style="light" />
        <Scr nav={nav} p={cur.p || {}} />
        {BAR.includes(cur.n) && <TabBar cur={TABS.includes(cur.n) ? cur.n : 'Home'} onPick={t => nav.reset(t)} />}
      </View>
    </Provider>
  );
}

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\README.md ====================

# NAVI MEAL (Expo Go)
1. `npm install`
2. `npx expo install expo-status-bar expo-font expo-location react-native-maps react-native-safe-area-context @react-native-async-storage/async-storage @expo/vector-icons @expo-google-fonts/poppins`
3. `npx expo start` and scan the QR code with Expo Go.
Tracking is a simulated rider (about 30 s). Data is local mock data in src/data.js.

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\app\cart.tsx ====================

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Image, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import BottomNav, { TabKey } from '../src/components/BottomNav';
import { BillRow, Card, EmptyState, RestaurantHeader } from '../src/components/Common';
import PrimaryButton from '../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import QuantityStepper from '../src/components/QuantityStepper';
import ScreenHeader from '../src/components/ScreenHeader';
import { DEFAULT_ADDRESS } from '../src/data/address';
import { restaurantById } from '../src/services/api';
import { useCart, useCartSummary } from '../src/store/cart';
import { useAnchor } from '../src/store/location';
import { toast } from '../src/store/ui';
import { colors, fonts } from '../src/theme';
import { rupee } from '../src/utils/format';

export function useTabNavigate() {
  const router = useRouter();
  return (k: TabKey) => router.navigate(k === 'index' ? '/(tabs)' : (`/${k}` as any));
}

export default function Cart() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const goTab = useTabNavigate();
  const anchor = useAnchor();
  const { items, count, bill, restaurantId } = useCartSummary();
  const increment = useCart((s) => s.increment);
  const decrement = useCart((s) => s.decrement);
  const r = useMemo(() => (restaurantId ? restaurantById(restaurantId, anchor) : undefined), [restaurantId, anchor.latitude, anchor.longitude]);

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }}>
      <ScreenHeader title="Your Cart" />
      {count === 0 || !r ? (
        <View style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 29 }}>
          <EmptyState icon="shopping-cart" title="Your cart is empty" subtitle="Add something tasty from a nearby restaurant." />
          <PrimaryButton title="Browse restaurants" height={50} radius={16} fontSize={18} onPress={() => router.replace('/(tabs)')} />
        </View>
      ) : (
        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 29, paddingBottom: 24 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
          <View style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
            <RestaurantHeader logo={r.logo} name={r.name} cuisine={r.cuisine} open={r.isOpen} size={72} />
          </View>

          {items.map(({ item, qty }) => (
            <Card key={item.id} style={styles.itemCard}>
              <Image source={item.image} style={styles.img} />
              <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 16 }}>
                <Text style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.price}>{rupee(item.price)}</Text>
                <View style={{ marginTop: 10, alignSelf: 'flex-start' }}>
                  <QuantityStepper qty={qty} onMinus={() => decrement(item.id)} onPlus={() => increment(item.id)} />
                </View>
              </View>
            </Card>
          ))}

          <Card style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>
            <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
              <Text style={styles.h}>Delivery Address</Text>
              <Pressable hitSlop={8} onPress={() => toast('Address book is coming soon')}>
                <Text style={styles.change}>Change ›</Text>
              </Pressable>
            </View>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 14 }}>
              <Feather name="map-pin" size={22} color={colors.cyan} />
              <View style={{ marginLeft: 14 }}>
                <Text style={styles.addrTitle}>{DEFAULT_ADDRESS.title}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.addrLine}>{DEFAULT_ADDRESS.line}</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </Card>

          <Card style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
            <Text style={styles.h}>Bill Details</Text>
            <BillRow label="Item Total" value={rupee(bill.subtotal)} />
            <BillRow label="Delivery Fee" value={rupee(bill.deliveryFee)} />
            <BillRow label="Discount" value={`−${rupee(bill.discount)}`} valueColor={colors.green} />
            <BillRow label="Taxes & charges" value={rupee(bill.taxes)} />
            <BillRow label="Total Amount" value={rupee(bill.total)} bold valueColor={colors.cyan} />
          </Card>

          <PrimaryButton title="Proceed to Checkout" style={{ marginTop: 16 }} onPress={() => router.push('/checkout')} />
        </ScrollView>
      )}
      <BottomNav active="index" onPress={goTab} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

import { useCartSummary } from '../src/store/cart';
import { useAnchor } from '../src/store/location';
import { toast } from '../src/store/ui';
import { colors, fonts } from '../src/theme';
import { rupee } from '../src/utils/format';
import { useTabNavigate } from './cart';

export default function Checkout() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const goTab = useTabNavigate();
  const anchor = useAnchor();
  const { items, count, bill, restaurantId } = useCartSummary();
  const r = useMemo(() => (restaurantId ? restaurantById(restaurantId, anchor) : undefined), [restaurantId, anchor.latitude, anchor.longitude]);

  if (count === 0 || !r) {
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }}>
        <ScreenHeader title="Checkout" />
        <View style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', padding: 29 }}>
          <Text style={{ fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 17, color: '#fff', textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>Your cart is empty</Text>
          <PrimaryButton title="Back to Home" height={50} radius={16} fontSize={18} onPress={() => router.replace('/(tabs)')} />
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }}>
      <ScreenHeader title="Checkout" />
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 29, paddingBottom: 24 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        <Card>
          <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
            <Text style={styles.h}>Delivery Address</Text>
            <Pressable hitSlop={8} onPress={() => toast('Address book is coming soon')}>

            </Pressable>
          </View>

            <Feather name="map-pin" size={22} color={colors.cyan} />

              <Text style={styles.addrTitle}>{DEFAULT_ADDRESS.title}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.addrLine}>{DEFAULT_ADDRESS.line}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Card>

        <Card style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
          <RestaurantHeader logo={r.logo} name={r.name} cuisine={r.cuisine} open={r.isOpen} size={66} />
          <Text style={[styles.h, { marginTop: 16 }]}>Order Items</Text>
          {items.map(({ item, qty }) => (
            <View key={item.id} style={styles.itemRow}>
              <Text style={styles.itemName}>{qty} × {item.name}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.itemPrice}>{rupee(item.price * qty)}</Text>
            </View>
          ))}
          <Text style={styles.note}>No cutlery needed • Deliver with care</Text>
        </Card>

        <Card style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
          <Text style={styles.h}>Payment Summary</Text>
          <BillRow label="Item Total" value={rupee(bill.subtotal)} />
          <BillRow label="Delivery Fee" value={rupee(bill.deliveryFee)} />
          <BillRow label="Taxes & charges" value={rupee(bill.taxes)} />
          <BillRow label="Discount" value={`−${rupee(bill.discount)}`} valueColor={colors.green} />

          <Text style={styles.estVal}>10–15 min</Text>
        </Card>

        <PrimaryButton title="Continue to Payment" style={{ marginTop: 16 }} onPress={() => router.push('/payment')} />
      </ScrollView>
      <BottomNav active="index" onPress={goTab} />
    </View>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { BillRow, Card, RestaurantHeader } from '../src/components/Common';
import PrimaryButton from '../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import { RESTAURANTS } from '../src/data/restaurants';
import { useOrders } from '../src/store/orders';
import { colors, fonts } from '../src/theme';
import { plural, rupee } from '../src/utils/format';

export default function Confirmed() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const router = useRouter();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const order = useOrders((s) => s.orders.find((o) => o.id === id));

  if (!order) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { justifyContent: 'center', padding: 29 }]}>
        <PrimaryButton title="Back to Home" onPress={() => router.replace('/(tabs)')} />
      </View>
    );
  }
  const logo = RESTAURANTS.find((r) => r.id === order.restaurantId)?.logo ?? null;
  const paidLabel = order.paymentMethod === 'COD' ? 'Total to pay on delivery' : `Total paid via ${order.paymentMethod === 'UPI' ? 'UPI' : 'Card'}`;

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ paddingTop: insets.top + 56, paddingHorizontal: 29, paddingBottom: 20 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        <View style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <View style={styles.outer}>
            <View style={styles.inner}>
              <Feather name="check" size={40} color={colors.green} />
            </View>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.title}>Order Confirmed!</Text>
          <Text style={styles.sub}>Your delicious order is being prepared by {order.restaurantName}.</Text>
          <View style={styles.pill}>
            <Text style={styles.pillText}>Order #{order.id}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <Card style={{ marginTop: 20, paddingVertical: 20 }}>
          <Text style={styles.small}>Estimated delivery time</Text>
          <Text style={styles.big}>10–15 min</Text>
          <Text style={styles.prep}>Restaurant is preparing your order</Text>
        </Card>

        <Card style={{ marginTop: 14, paddingBottom: 20 }}>
          <RestaurantHeader logo={logo} name={order.restaurantName} cuisine={order.restaurantCuisine} size={66} />
          <View style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>
            <BillRow label={plural(order.itemCount, 'item')} value={rupee(order.subtotal)} />
            <BillRow label="Delivery + taxes" value={rupee(order.deliveryFee + order.taxes)} />
            <BillRow label="Discount" value={`−${rupee(order.discount)}`} valueColor={colors.green} />
            <BillRow label={paidLabel} value={rupee(order.total)} bold valueColor={colors.cyan} />
          </View>
        </Card>
      </ScrollView>

      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 29, paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 14) + 4, paddingTop: 10 }}>
        <PrimaryButton title="Track Order" height={56} onPress={() => router.replace({ pathname: '/track', params: { id: order.id } })} />
        <Text style={styles.home} onPress={() => router.replace('/(tabs)')}>Back to Home</Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg },
  outer: { width: 176, height: 176, borderRadius: 88, borderWidth: 3, borderColor: colors.green, backgroundColor: '#0E3E2B', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  inner: { width: 84, height: 84, borderRadius: 42, borderWidth: 3, borderColor: colors.green, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  title: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 30, color: '#fff', marginTop: 26 },
  sub: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 15, color: colors.textSoft, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 14, lineHeight: 23, paddingHorizontal: 8 },
  pill: { backgroundColor: '#101C2E', borderRadius: 20, paddingHorizontal: 22, paddingVertical: 9, marginTop: 18 },
  pillText: { fontFamily: fonts.medium, fontSize: 15, color: colors.cyan },

  big: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 34, color: '#fff', marginTop: 4 },

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { Image, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import GradientBg from '../src/components/GradientBg';
import PrimaryButton from '../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import { useHydrated } from '../src/hooks/useHydrated';
import { useAuth } from '../src/store/auth';
import { useLocationStore } from '../src/store/location';

export default function Splash() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const ready = useHydrated();
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  const onboarded = useAuth((s) => s.onboarded);
  const asked = useLocationStore((s) => s.asked);

  // Returning, signed-in users skip straight into the app.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready || !user) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => router.replace(asked ? '/(tabs)' : '/location'), 1400);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [ready, user, asked, router]);

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <GradientBg variant="splash" />
      <Image source={require('../assets/images/logo.png')} style={styles.logo} resizeMode="contain" />
      {ready && !user ? (
        <PrimaryButton
          title="Get Started"
          style={styles.btn}
          height={50}
          radius={14}
          fontSize={19}
          onPress={() => router.replace(onboarded ? '/login' : '/onboarding')}
        />
      ) : (
        <View style={{ height: 50, marginTop: 56 }} />
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#02060F', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  logo: { width: 300, height: 300 },
  btn: { width: 265, marginTop: 56 },
});



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\app\location.tsx ====================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Image, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import AuthShell from '../src/components/AuthShell';
import PrimaryButton from '../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import { colors, fonts } from '../src/theme';
import { useLocationStore } from '../src/store/location';
import { toast } from '../src/store/ui';

export default function LocationPermission() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const allow = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    const ok = await useLocationStore.getState().requestPermission();
    setBusy(false);
    if (!ok) toast('Location is off - showing restaurants around Anna Nagar');
    router.replace('/(tabs)');
  };
  const notNow = () => {
    useLocationStore.getState().markAsked();
    router.replace('/(tabs)');
  };

  return (
    <AuthShell scroll={false}>
      <View style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        <Image source={require('../assets/images/location.jpg')} style={styles.img} />
        <Text style={styles.title}>Find Food Near You</Text>
        <Text style={styles.body}>
          Allow location access to discover restaurants, get accurate delivery times, and track your order in real time.
        </Text>
      </View>
      <PrimaryButton title="Allow Location" height={50} radius={14} fontSize={20} loading={busy} onPress={allow} />
      <Pressable onPress={notNow} style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 16 }} hitSlop={10}>
        <Text style={styles.notNow}>Not Now</Text>
      </Pressable>
    </AuthShell>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  img: { width: 150, height: 150, borderRadius: 24, marginBottom: 28 },
  title: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 22, color: colors.text, textAlign: 'center' },
  body: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 23, color: colors.text, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 14, paddingHorizontal: 14 },
  notNow: { fontFamily: fonts.medium, fontSize: 13, color: colors.textSoft },
});



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\app\login.tsx ====================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Image, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import AuthShell from '../src/components/AuthShell';
import PrimaryButton from '../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import TextField from '../src/components/TextField';
import { colors, fonts } from '../src/theme';
import { login } from '../src/services/auth';
import { useAuth } from '../src/store/auth';
import { useLocationStore } from '../src/store/location';
import { toast } from '../src/store/ui';

export default function Login() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const setUser = useAuth((s) => s.setUser);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [pw, setPw] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async () => {
    if (!email.trim() || !pw) {
      setError('Enter your email and password');
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const user = await login(email, pw);
      setUser(user);
      router.replace(useLocationStore.getState().asked ? '/(tabs)' : '/location');
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err?.message ?? 'Login failed');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AuthShell>
      <Text style={styles.title}>Welcome Back 👋</Text>
      <Text style={styles.sub}>Log in to continue your food journey.</Text>
      <View style={{ marginTop: 26 }}>
        <TextField label="Email" icon="mail" placeholder="Enter your email" value={email} onChangeText={setEmail} keyboardType="email-address" />
        <TextField label="Password" icon="lock" placeholder="Enter password" value={pw} onChangeText={setPw} secure error={error} />
        <Pressable style={{ alignSelf: 'flex-end', marginTop: -4 }} onPress={() => toast('Password reset is coming soon')} hitSlop={8}>
          <Text style={styles.forgot}>Forgot Password?</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
      <PrimaryButton title="Log In" height={52} radius={16} fontSize={22} loading={busy} onPress={submit} style={{ marginTop: 26 }} />
      <View style={styles.orRow}>
        <View style={styles.line} />
        <Text style={styles.or}>OR</Text>
        <View style={styles.line} />
      </View>
      <Pressable style={styles.google} onPress={() => toast('Google sign-in is not configured yet')}>
        <Image source={require('../assets/images/google.png')} style={{ width: 26, height: 26 }} />
        <Text style={styles.googleText}>Continue with Google</Text>
      </Pressable>
      <View style={styles.foot}>
        <Text style={styles.footText}>Don't have an account?</Text>
        <Pressable onPress={() => router.replace('/signup')} hitSlop={8}>
          <Text style={styles.link}> Sign Up</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    </AuthShell>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  title: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 26, color: colors.text, textAlign: 'center' },
  sub: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 13, color: colors.text, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 2 },
  forgot: { fontFamily: fonts.medium, fontSize: 12, color: colors.cyan },
  orRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 8, marginVertical: 12 },
  line: { height: 1, width: 62, backgroundColor: '#1E3B66' },
  or: { fontFamily: fonts.medium, fontSize: 12, color: colors.cyan },
  google: { height: 52, borderRadius: 16, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#3A4558', backgroundColor: '#1D2634', flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 12 },
  googleText: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 18, color: colors.text },
  foot: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'center', marginTop: 14 },
  footText: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 12.5, color: colors.textSoft },
  link: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 12.5, color: colors.cyan },
});



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\app\onboarding.tsx ====================

import React, { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Image, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View, useWindowDimensions } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import GradientBg from '../src/components/GradientBg';
import PrimaryButton from '../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import { colors, fonts } from '../src/theme';
import { useAuth } from '../src/store/auth';

const PAGES = [
  { title: 'Discover Food Around You', body: 'Explore nearby restaurants on an interactive map and find your next favourite meal.', img: require('../assets/images/onb1.jpg'), w: 312, h: 218, fit: 'cover' as const },
  { title: 'Choose What Fits You', body: 'Compare ratings, distance, delivery time, and food options to find the perfect place for you.', img: require('../assets/images/onb2.jpg'), w: 220, h: 196, fit: 'cover' as const },
  { title: 'Track Your Order Live', body: 'Follow your order in real time from the restaurant to your doorstep.', img: require('../assets/images/onb3.jpg'), w: 220, h: 220, fit: 'cover' as const },
];

export default function Onboarding() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { width, height } = useWindowDimensions();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const finish = useAuth((s) => s.finishOnboarding);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const ref = useRef<FlatList>(null);
  const cardW = width - 44;

  const done = () => {
    finish();
    router.replace('/signup');
  };
  const next = () => {
    if (index >= PAGES.length - 1) return done();
    ref.current?.scrollToIndex({ index: index + 1, animated: true });
  };

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#02060F' }}>
      <GradientBg variant="card" />
      <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top + 8, height * 0.12), paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom + 16, height * 0.1), paddingHorizontal: 22 }}>
        <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 14 }}>
          <Image source={require('../assets/images/logo.png')} style={{ width: 72, height: 72 }} resizeMode="contain" />
        </View>
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Pressable style={styles.skip} hitSlop={12} onPress={done}>
            <Text style={styles.skipText}>skip</Text>
          </Pressable>
          <FlatList
            ref={ref}
            data={PAGES}
            horizontal
            pagingEnabled
            showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
            keyExtractor={(p) => p.title}
            onMomentumScrollEnd={(e) => setIndex(Math.round(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.x / cardW))}
            getItemLayout={(_, i) => ({ length: cardW, offset: cardW * i, index: i })}
            style={{ flexGrow: 0 }}
            renderItem={({ item }) => (
              <View style={{ width: cardW, alignItems: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 22 }}>
                <Text style={styles.title}>{item.title}</Text>
                <Image source={item.img} style={[styles.img, { width: item.w, height: item.h }]} resizeMode={item.fit} />
                <Text style={styles.body}>{item.body}</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          />
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }} />
          <View style={styles.dots}>
            {PAGES.map((_, i) => (
              <View key={i} style={[styles.dot, i === index && styles.dotOn]} />
            ))}
          </View>
          <PrimaryButton
            title={index === PAGES.length - 1 ? 'Get Started →' : 'Next →'}
            height={50}
            radius={16}
            fontSize={21}
            style={{ marginHorizontal: 22 }}
            textStyle={{ fontFamily: fonts.bold }}
            onPress={next}
          />
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  card: { flex: 1, borderRadius: 40, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#15294A', backgroundColor: 'rgba(7,15,32,0.72)', paddingTop: 24, paddingBottom: 24, overflow: 'hidden' },
  skip: { position: 'absolute', right: 32, top: 22, zIndex: 2 },
  skipText: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 13, color: '#B7C3DA' },
  title: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 21, color: colors.text, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 28, marginBottom: 22 },
  img: { borderRadius: 12, borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: colors.cyan, marginBottom: 22 },
  body: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 15.5, lineHeight: 24, color: colors.text, textAlign: 'center' },
  dots: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 6, marginBottom: 22 },
  dot: { width: 11, height: 11, borderRadius: 6, borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: colors.cyan },

import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather, MaterialCommunityIcons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import PrimaryButton from '../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import ScreenHeader from '../src/components/ScreenHeader';
import { useCartSummary } from '../src/store/cart';
import { useCheckout } from '../src/store/checkout';
import { colors, fonts } from '../src/theme';
import { PaymentMethod } from '../src/types';
import { rupee } from '../src/utils/format';

const METHODS: { key: PaymentMethod; title: string; sub: string; icon: any }[] = [
  { key: 'UPI', title: 'UPI', sub: 'Google Pay, PhonePe, Paytm', icon: 'wallet-outline' },
  { key: 'CARD', title: 'Credit / Debit Card', sub: 'Visa, Mastercard, RuPay', icon: 'credit-card-outline' },
  { key: 'COD', title: 'Cash on Delivery', sub: 'Pay when your order arrives', icon: 'cash' },
];

export default function Payment() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { bill, count } = useCartSummary();
  const method = useCheckout((s) => s.method);
  const setMethod = useCheckout((s) => s.setMethod);

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }}>
      <ScreenHeader title="Payment" />
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 29 }}>
        <Text style={styles.h}>Choose payment method</Text>
        {METHODS.map((m) => {
          const on = m.key === method;
          return (
            <Pressable key={m.key} onPress={() => setMethod(m.key)} style={[styles.card, on && styles.cardOn]}>
              <MaterialCommunityIcons name={m.icon} size={28} color={colors.cyan} />
              <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 16 }}>
                <Text style={styles.title}>{m.title}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.sub}>{m.sub}</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.radio, on && { borderColor: colors.cyan }]}>{on ? <View style={styles.radioDot} /> : null}</View>
            </Pressable>
          );
        })}

        <View style={styles.totals}>
          <View style={styles.row}>
            <Text style={styles.lbl}>Order total</Text>
            <Text style={styles.val}>{rupee(bill.total)}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.row, { marginTop: 14 }]}>
            <Text style={[styles.lbl, { color: '#fff', fontFamily: fonts.semi }]}>Amount to pay</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.val, { color: colors.cyan, fontFamily: fonts.semi }]}>{method === 'COD' ? '₹0 now' : rupee(bill.total)}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, marginTop: 14 }}>
          <Feather name="shield" size={22} color={colors.green} />
          <Text style={styles.secure}>Payments are secure and encrypted</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={{ flex: 1 }} />
      <PrimaryButton
        title={method === 'COD' ? `Place Order  ${rupee(bill.total)}` : `Pay Now  ${rupee(bill.total)}`}
        style={{ marginHorizontal: 29, marginBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 16) + 8 }}
        disabled={count === 0}
        height={56}
        onPress={() => router.push('/processing')}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\app\processing.tsx ====================

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Animated, Easing, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import PrimaryButton from '../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import { DEFAULT_ADDRESS } from '../src/data/address';
import { payMock } from '../src/services/payment';
import { placeOrder } from '../src/services/orders';
import { restaurantById } from '../src/services/api';
import { useCart } from '../src/store/cart';
import { useCheckout } from '../src/store/checkout';
import { FALLBACK_LOCATION, colors, fonts } from '../src/theme';
import { useLocationStore } from '../src/store/location';
import { useOrders } from '../src/store/orders';
import { calcBill } from '../src/utils/bill';
import { getMenuItem } from '../src/data/menu';
import { rupee } from '../src/utils/format';

export default function Processing() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const spin = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const started = useRef(false);
  const method = useCheckout((s) => s.method);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);

  // amount is computed once, from the cart, when this screen opens
  const total = useRef(
    calcBill(
      Object.entries(useCart.getState().lines).reduce((n, [id, q]) => n + (getMenuItem(id)?.price ?? 0) * q, 0)
    ).total
  ).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    Animated.loop(Animated.timing(spin, { toValue: 1, duration: 1100, easing: Easing.linear, useNativeDriver: true })).start();
  }, [spin]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (started.current && attempt === 0) return;
    started.current = true;
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      const { lines, restaurantId } = useCart.getState();
      const loc = useLocationStore.getState().coords ?? FALLBACK_LOCATION;
      const restaurant = restaurantId ? restaurantById(restaurantId, loc) : undefined;
      if (!restaurant || Object.keys(lines).length === 0) {
        router.replace('/(tabs)');
        return;
      }
      try {
        const [pay, order] = await Promise.all([
          payMock(method, total),
          placeOrder({ restaurant, lines, method, dest: loc, addressTitle: DEFAULT_ADDRESS.title, addressLine: DEFAULT_ADDRESS.line }),
        ]);
        if (cancelled) return;
        if (!pay.ok) {
          setError(pay.reason);
          return;
        }
        useOrders.getState().addOrder(order);
        useCart.getState().clear();
        router.replace({ pathname: '/confirmed', params: { id: order.id } });
      } catch (e: any) {
        if (!cancelled) setError(e?.message ?? 'Something went wrong');
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [attempt]);

  const rotate = spin.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: ['0deg', '360deg'] });
  const cod = method === 'COD';

  if (error) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.root}>
        <View style={[styles.iconCircle, { borderColor: colors.red, backgroundColor: colors.redBg }]}>
          <Feather name="x" size={44} color={colors.red} />
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.title}>Payment failed</Text>
        <Text style={styles.sub}>{error}</Text>
        <PrimaryButton title="Try Again" style={{ width: 240, marginTop: 28 }} height={50} fontSize={18} onPress={() => { setError(null); setAttempt((a) => a + 1); }} />
        <Text style={styles.back} onPress={() => router.back()}>Back to Payment</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <View style={styles.ringWrap}>
        <View style={styles.ringBase} />
        <Animated.View style={[styles.ring, { transform: [{ rotate }] }]} />
        <Feather name="shield" size={34} color={colors.cyan} />
      </View>
      <Text style={styles.title}>{cod ? 'Placing your order...' : 'Processing Payment...'}</Text>
      <Text style={styles.amount}>{rupee(total)}</Text>
      <Text style={styles.sub}>Please don't close the app or go back.</Text>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 30 },
  ringWrap: { width: 120, height: 120, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', marginBottom: 32 },
  ringBase: { position: 'absolute', width: 120, height: 120, borderRadius: 60, borderWidth: 5, borderColor: '#14223A' },
  ring: { position: 'absolute', width: 120, height: 120, borderRadius: 60, borderWidth: 5, borderColor: 'transparent', borderTopColor: colors.cyan },
  iconCircle: { width: 110, height: 110, borderRadius: 55, borderWidth: 3, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', marginBottom: 28 },
  title: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 24, color: '#fff', textAlign: 'center' },
  amount: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 30, color: colors.cyan, marginTop: 10 },
  sub: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 13.5, color: colors.muted, marginTop: 10, textAlign: 'center' },
  back: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 15, color: colors.cyan, marginTop: 20 },
});



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\app\signup.tsx ====================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import AuthShell from '../src/components/AuthShell';
import PrimaryButton from '../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import TextField from '../src/components/TextField';
import { colors, fonts } from '../src/theme';
import { register } from '../src/services/auth';
import { useAuth } from '../src/store/auth';
import { useLocationStore } from '../src/store/location';

const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

export default function Signup() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const setUser = useAuth((s) => s.setUser);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [pw, setPw] = useState('');
  const [pw2, setPw2] = useState('');
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async () => {
    const e: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (name.trim().length < 2) e.name = 'Please enter your full name';
    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email.trim())) e.email = 'Enter a valid email address';
    if (pw.length < 6) e.pw = 'Use at least 6 characters';
    if (pw2 !== pw) e.pw2 = 'Passwords do not match';
    setErrors(e);
    if (Object.keys(e).length) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const user = await register(name, email, pw);
      setUser(user);
      router.replace(useLocationStore.getState().asked ? '/(tabs)' : '/location');
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrors({ email: err?.message ?? 'Could not create account' });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AuthShell>
      <Text style={styles.title}>Create Your Account</Text>
      <Text style={styles.sub}>Join Navi Meal and discover food around you.</Text>
      <View style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
        <TextField label="Full Name" icon="user" placeholder="Enter your name" value={name} onChangeText={setName} autoCapitalize="words" error={errors.name} />
        <TextField label="Email" icon="mail" placeholder="Enter your email" value={email} onChangeText={setEmail} keyboardType="email-address" error={errors.email} />
        <TextField label="Password" icon="lock" placeholder="Create a password" value={pw} onChangeText={setPw} secure error={errors.pw} />
        <TextField label="Confirm Password" icon="lock" placeholder="Confirm password" value={pw2} onChangeText={setPw2} secure error={errors.pw2} />
      </View>
      <PrimaryButton title="Create Account" height={52} radius={16} fontSize={21} loading={busy} onPress={submit} style={{ marginTop: 10 }} />
      <View style={styles.foot}>
        <Text style={styles.footText}>Already have an account?</Text>
        <Pressable onPress={() => router.replace('/login')} hitSlop={8}>
          <Text style={styles.link}> Login In</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    </AuthShell>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  title: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 24, color: colors.text, textAlign: 'center' },
  sub: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 12.5, color: colors.text, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 2 },
  foot: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'center', marginTop: 18 },
  footText: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 12.5, color: colors.textSoft },
  link: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 12.5, color: colors.cyan },
});

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import GradientBg from '../../src/components/GradientBg';
import { EmptyState } from '../../src/components/Common';
import { ListRow, PopularCard } from '../../src/components/RestaurantCards';
import { CATEGORY_CHIPS } from '../../src/data/restaurants';
import { nearbyRestaurants } from '../../src/services/api';
import { useAnchor } from '../../src/store/location';
import { colors, fonts } from '../../src/theme';
import { toast } from '../../src/store/ui';

const POPULAR = ['pizza-corner', 'burger-house', 'morning-brew'];

export default function Explore() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { focus } = useLocalSearchParams<{ focus?: string }>();
  const anchor = useAnchor();
  const inputRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const [chip, setChip] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focus) setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 250);
  }, [focus]);

  const all = useMemo(() => nearbyRestaurants(anchor), [anchor.latitude, anchor.longitude]);
  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const term = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    return all.filter((r) => {
      const hay = `${r.name} ${r.cuisine} ${r.tags.join(' ')}`.toLowerCase();
      const okQ = !term || hay.includes(term);
      const okC = !chip || r.tags.some((t) => t.toLowerCase() === chip.toLowerCase());
      return okQ && okC;
    });
  }, [all, q, chip]);

  const popular = filtered.filter((r) => POPULAR.includes(r.id));
  const nearby = filtered.filter((r) => !POPULAR.includes(r.id)).sort((a, b) => a.distanceKm - b.distanceKm);
  const open = (id: string) => router.push({ pathname: '/restaurant/[id]', params: { id } });

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#030814' }}>
      <GradientBg variant="tab" />
      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingTop: insets.top + 14, paddingBottom: 30 }}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      >
        <View style={styles.searchRow}>
          <View style={styles.search}>
            <Feather name="search" size={22} color={colors.muted} />
            <TextInput
              ref={inputRef}
              value={q}
              onChangeText={setQ}
              placeholder="Find foods or restaurants..."
              placeholderTextColor={colors.muted}
              style={styles.input}
              returnKeyType="search"
            />
            {q ? (
              <Pressable hitSlop={10} onPress={() => setQ('')}>
                <Feather name="x" size={18} color={colors.muted} />
              </Pressable>
            ) : null}
          </View>
          <Pressable style={styles.filter} onPress={() => toast('More filters are coming soon')}>
            <Feather name="filter" size={22} color={colors.cyan} />
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 24, gap: 10, paddingVertical: 14 }}>
          {CATEGORY_CHIPS.map((c) => {
            const on = chip === c;
            return (
              <Pressable key={c} onPress={() => setChip(on ? null : c)} style={[styles.chip, on && styles.chipOn]}>
                <Text style={[styles.chipText, on && { color: colors.onCyan, fontFamily: fonts.semi }]}>{c}</Text>
              </Pressable>
            );
          })}
        </ScrollView>

        {filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState title="No restaurants found" subtitle="Try a different search or category." />
        ) : (
          <>
            {popular.length > 0 && (
              <>
                <Text style={styles.section}>Popular Restaurants</Text>
                <FlatList
                  horizontal
                  data={popular}
                  keyExtractor={(r) => r.id}
                  showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
                  contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 24, paddingBottom: 6 }}
                  renderItem={({ item }) => <PopularCard r={item} onPress={() => open(item.id)} />}
                />
              </>
            )}
            {nearby.length > 0 && (
              <>
                <Text style={[styles.section, { marginTop: 22 }]}>Nearby Restaurants</Text>
                <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 24 }}>
                  {nearby.map((r) => (
                    <ListRow key={r.id} r={r} onPress={() => open(r.id)} />
                  ))}
                </View>
              </>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  searchRow: { flexDirection: 'row', paddingHorizontal: 24, gap: 12 },
  search: { flex: 1, height: 52, borderRadius: 16, backgroundColor: '#12203A', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.cardBorder, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 16, gap: 12 },
  input: { flex: 1, fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 14, color: colors.text, paddingVertical: 0 },
  filter: { width: 52, height: 52, borderRadius: 16, backgroundColor: '#12203A', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.cardBorder, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  chip: { height: 40, paddingHorizontal: 18, borderRadius: 20, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#243248', backgroundColor: '#0A1424', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  chipOn: { backgroundColor: colors.cyan, borderColor: colors.cyan },
  chipText: { fontFamily: fonts.medium, fontSize: 14, color: colors.text },
  section: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 19, color: colors.text, paddingHorizontal: 24, marginBottom: 12 },
});



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\app\(tabs)\index.tsx ====================

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import MapView, { Circle } from 'react-native-maps';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { HomeCard } from '../../src/components/RestaurantCards';
import { PinMarker, RestaurantPin, UserDot } from '../../src/components/MapPins';
import { colors, fonts } from '../../src/theme';
import { nearbyRestaurants } from '../../src/services/api';
import { useAnchor, useLocationStore } from '../../src/store/location';
import { toast } from '../../src/store/ui';

const CARD_W = 132 + 12;

export default function Home() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const anchor = useAnchor();
  const placeName = useLocationStore((s) => s.placeName);
  const refresh = useLocationStore((s) => s.refresh);
  const mapRef = useRef<MapView>(null);
  const listRef = useRef<FlatList>(null);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const restaurants = useMemo(() => nearbyRestaurants(anchor), [anchor.latitude, anchor.longitude]);

  const region = useMemo(
    () => ({ latitude: anchor.latitude - 0.0038, longitude: anchor.longitude, latitudeDelta: 0.052, longitudeDelta: 0.036 }),
    [anchor.latitude, anchor.longitude]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    refresh();
  }, [refresh]);

  useEffect(() => {
    mapRef.current?.animateToRegion(region, 600);
  }, [region]);

  const open = (id: string) => router.push({ pathname: '/restaurant/[id]', params: { id } });

  const onPin = (id: string, index: number) => {
    if (selected === id) return open(id); // second tap opens the restaurant
    setSelected(id);
    listRef.current?.scrollToIndex({ index, animated: true, viewPosition: 0 });
  };

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bgHome }}>
      <MapView
        ref={mapRef}
        style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
        initialRegion={region}
        toolbarEnabled={false}
        showsCompass={false}
        showsPointsOfInterest
        rotateEnabled={false}
        onPress={() => setSelected(null)}
      >
        <Circle center={anchor} radius={520} fillColor="rgba(47,123,255,0.20)" strokeColor="rgba(47,123,255,0.28)" strokeWidth={1} />
        <PinMarker coordinate={anchor} zIndex={5}>
          <UserDot />
        </PinMarker>
        {restaurants.map((r, i) => (
          <PinMarker key={r.id} coordinate={r.coords} anchor={{ x: 0.5, y: 0.3 }} zIndex={selected === r.id ? 10 : 1} onPress={() => onPin(r.id, i)}>
            <RestaurantPin color={r.pinColor} icon={r.pinIcon} minutes={r.deliveryMin} rating={r.rating} selected={selected === r.id} />
          </PinMarker>
        ))}
      </MapView>

      {/* top panel */}
      <View style={[styles.top, { paddingTop: insets.top + 8 }]}>
        <View style={styles.topRow}>
          <Ionicons name="location" size={30} color={colors.cyan} />
          <Pressable style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 8 }} onPress={() => toast('Delivery address picker is coming soon')}>
            <Text style={styles.deliver}>Deliver to</Text>
            <Text style={styles.place} numberOfLines={1}>
              {placeName} <Feather name="chevron-down" size={13} color="#fff" />
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
          <Pressable hitSlop={10} onPress={() => toast('No new notifications')} style={{ marginRight: 18 }}>
            <Feather name="bell" size={25} color="#fff" />
            <View style={styles.dot} />
          </Pressable>
          <Pressable hitSlop={10} onPress={() => router.navigate('/profile')}>
            <Feather name="user" size={25} color="#fff" />
          </Pressable>
        </View>
        <Pressable style={styles.search} onPress={() => router.navigate({ pathname: '/explore', params: { focus: String(Date.now()) } })}>
          <Feather name="search" size={22} color="#C9D2E0" />
          <Text style={styles.searchText}>Search restaurants or food</Text>
          <View style={styles.sep} />
          <Feather name="filter" size={22} color="#C9D2E0" />
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      {/* locate-me button */}
      <Pressable style={styles.locate} onPress={() => mapRef.current?.animateToRegion(region, 500)}>
        <Ionicons name="location-outline" size={24} color="#2F7BFF" />
      </Pressable>

      {/* bottom sheet */}
      <View style={styles.sheet}>
        <View style={styles.sheetHead}>
          <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>Nearby Restaurants</Text>
          <Pressable hitSlop={10} onPress={() => router.navigate('/explore')}>
            <Text style={styles.seeAll}>See All  ›</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
        <FlatList
          ref={listRef}
          data={restaurants}
          horizontal
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          keyExtractor={(r) => r.id}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 16, gap: 12, paddingBottom: 14 }}
          getItemLayout={(_, i) => ({ length: CARD_W, offset: 16 + CARD_W * i, index: i })}
          renderItem={({ item }) => <HomeCard r={item} selected={selected === item.id} onPress={() => open(item.id)} />}
        />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

import React from 'react';
import { Image, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import GradientBg from '../../src/components/GradientBg';
import { Chip, EmptyState } from '../../src/components/Common';
import PrimaryButton from '../../src/components/PrimaryButton';
import { RESTAURANTS } from '../../src/data/restaurants';
import { useNow } from '../../src/hooks/useNow';
import { deliveryProvider } from '../../src/services/delivery/SimulatedDeliveryProvider';
import { isActive, useOrders } from '../../src/store/orders';
import { colors, fonts } from '../../src/theme';
import { Order } from '../../src/types';
import { formatDate, plural, rupee } from '../../src/utils/format';
import { toast } from '../../src/store/ui';

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  PLACED: 'Placed', CONFIRMED: 'Confirmed', PREPARING: 'Preparing', READY_FOR_PICKUP: 'Ready', PICKED_UP: 'Picked up', ON_THE_WAY: 'On the way',
};

export default function Orders() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const orders = useOrders((s) => s.orders);
  const active = orders.filter(isActive);
  const past = orders.filter((o) => !isActive(o));
  const now = useNow(1000, active.length > 0);

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#030814' }}>
      <GradientBg variant="tab" />
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ paddingTop: insets.top + 18, paddingBottom: 30, paddingHorizontal: 24 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        <View style={styles.head}>
          <Text style={styles.title}>My Orders</Text>
          <Pressable hitSlop={10} onPress={() => toast('No new notifications')}>
            <Feather name="bell" size={34} color="#fff" />
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        {active.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <Text style={styles.section}>ACTIVE ORDER</Text>
            {active.map((o) => {
              const st = deliveryProvider.getStatus(o, now);
              const logo = RESTAURANTS.find((r) => r.id === o.restaurantId)?.logo;
              return (
                <View key={o.id} style={styles.activeCard}>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    <View style={styles.logo}>{logo ? <Image source={logo} style={{ width: 40, height: 40 }} resizeMode="contain" /> : null}</View>
                    <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 12 }}>
                      <Text style={styles.name}>{o.restaurantName}</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.sub}>{plural(o.itemCount, 'item')} • {rupee(o.total)}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <Chip label={STATUS_LABEL[st.status] ?? st.status} tone="blue" />
                  </View>
                  <View style={styles.etaRow}>
                    <Text style={styles.eta}>Arriving in {st.etaMin} mins</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.near}>Near {o.addressTitle.split(',')[0]}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={styles.barBg}>
                    <View style={[styles.barFill, { width: `${Math.max(6, st.overall * 100)}%` }]} />
                  </View>
                  <PrimaryButton title="Track Order" height={44} radius={12} fontSize={15} style={{ marginTop: 14 }} onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/track', params: { id: o.id } })} />
                </View>
              );
            })}
          </>
        )}

        <Text style={[styles.section, { marginTop: 22 }]}>PAST ORDERS</Text>
        {past.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState icon="shopping-bag" title="No past orders yet" subtitle="Your delivered and cancelled orders will show up here." />
        ) : (
          past.map((o) => <PastCard key={o.id} o={o} onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/order/[id]', params: { id: o.id } })} />)
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

function PastCard({ o, onPress }: { o: Order; onPress: () => void }) {
  const delivered = o.status === 'DELIVERED';
  return (
    <Pressable onPress={onPress} style={styles.pastCard}>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
        <View>
          <Text style={styles.pastName}>{o.restaurantName}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.pastSub}>{formatDate(o.createdAt)} • {plural(o.itemCount, 'item')}</Text>
        </View>
        <Chip label={delivered ? 'Delivered' : 'Cancelled'} tone={delivered ? 'green' : 'red'} />
      </View>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 14 }}>
        <Text style={styles.total}>{rupee(o.total)}</Text>
        <Text style={styles.viewDetails}>View Details ›</Text>
      </View>
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  head: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 22 },
  title: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 26, color: '#fff' },
  section: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 14, color: colors.cyan, letterSpacing: 0.6, marginBottom: 10 },
  activeCard: { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderRadius: 18, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.cardBorder, padding: 18 },

  sub: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 12.5, color: colors.muted },

});

import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import GradientBg from '../../src/components/GradientBg';
import { useAuth } from '../../src/store/auth';
import { colors, fonts } from '../../src/theme';
import { toast } from '../../src/store/ui';

const ROWS: { icon: keyof typeof Feather.glyphMap; label: string }[] = [
  { icon: 'user', label: 'Personal Information' },
  { icon: 'map-pin', label: 'Saved Addresses' },
  { icon: 'credit-card', label: 'Payment Methods' },
  { icon: 'heart', label: 'Favorites' },
  { icon: 'bell', label: 'Notifications' },
  { icon: 'help-circle', label: 'Help & Support' },
  { icon: 'settings', label: 'Settings' },
];

export default function Profile() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  const logout = useAuth((s) => s.logout);
  const initial = (user?.name ?? 'N').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase();

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#030814' }}>
      <GradientBg variant="tab" />
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ paddingTop: insets.top + 24, paddingBottom: 30, paddingHorizontal: 24 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        <View style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <View style={styles.avatar}>
            <Text style={styles.initial}>{initial}</Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.name}>{user?.name}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.email}>{user?.email}</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ marginTop: 34 }}>
          {ROWS.map((r) => (
            <Pressable key={r.label} style={styles.row} onPress={() => toast(`${r.label} is coming soon`)}>
              <Feather name={r.icon} size={21} color={colors.cyan} />
              <Text style={styles.rowText}>{r.label}</Text>
              <Feather name="chevron-right" size={16} color={colors.muted} />
            </Pressable>
          ))}
          <Pressable
            style={[styles.row, styles.logout]}
            onPress={() => {
              logout();
              router.replace('/login');
            }}
          >
            <View style={{ flex: 1, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
              <Feather name="log-out" size={19} color={colors.red} />
              <Text style={[styles.rowText, { color: colors.red, flex: 0, marginLeft: 0, fontFamily: fonts.bold }]}>Log Out</Text>
            </View>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\app\(tabs)\_layout.tsx ====================

import React from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { Redirect, Tabs } from 'expo-router';
import BottomNav, { TabKey } from '../../src/components/BottomNav';
import CartBar from '../../src/components/CartBar';
import { useAuth } from '../../src/store/auth';

export default function TabsLayout() {
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  if (!user) return <Redirect href="/login" />;
  return (
    <Tabs
      screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }}
      tabBar={(props) => {
        const active = props.state.routes[props.state.index].name as TabKey;
        return (
          <View>
            {active === 'index' || active === 'explore' ? <CartBar /> : null}
            <BottomNav active={active} onPress={(k) => props.navigation.navigate(k)} />
          </View>
        );
      }}
    >
      <Tabs.Screen name="index" />
      <Tabs.Screen name="explore" />
      <Tabs.Screen name="orders" />
      <Tabs.Screen name="profile" />
    </Tabs>
  );
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\app\restaurant\[id].tsx ====================

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Alert, Image, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather, MaterialCommunityIcons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import CartBar from '../../src/components/CartBar';
import { Chip, EmptyState } from '../../src/components/Common';
import { AddButton } from '../../src/components/QuantityStepper';
import QuantityStepper from '../../src/components/QuantityStepper';
import { getMenuItem } from '../../src/data/menu';
import { menuForRestaurant, restaurantById } from '../../src/services/api';
import { useCart } from '../../src/store/cart';
import { useFavorites } from '../../src/store/favorites';
import { useAnchor } from '../../src/store/location';
import { toast } from '../../src/store/ui';
import { colors, fonts } from '../../src/theme';
import { MenuItem } from '../../src/types';

export default function RestaurantDetails() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const router = useRouter();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const anchor = useAnchor();
  const r = useMemo(() => restaurantById(String(id), anchor), [id, anchor.latitude, anchor.longitude]);
  const menu = useMemo(() => menuForRestaurant(String(id)), [id]);
  const lines = useCart((s) => s.lines);
  const cartRestaurant = useCart((s) => s.restaurantId);
  const add = useCart((s) => s.add);
  const replaceWith = useCart((s) => s.replaceWith);
  const increment = useCart((s) => s.increment);
  const decrement = useCart((s) => s.decrement);
  const fav = useFavorites((s) => (r ? s.ids.includes(r.id) : false));
  const toggleFav = useFavorites((s) => s.toggle);

  if (!r) {
    return (
      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg, paddingTop: insets.top + 20 }}>
        <Pressable style={{ padding: 20 }} onPress={() => router.back()}>
          <Feather name="arrow-left" size={28} color={colors.cyan} />
        </Pressable>
        <EmptyState icon="alert-circle" title="Restaurant not found" />
      </View>
    );
  }

  const onAdd = (item: MenuItem) => {
    if (!r.isOpen || !item.available) return toast('This item is not available right now');
    if (add(item)) return;
    const other = cartRestaurant ? menuOwnerName(cartRestaurant) : 'another restaurant';
    Alert.alert('Start a new cart?', `Your cart has items from ${other}. Adding this will clear it.`, [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Replace', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => replaceWith(item) },
    ]);
  };

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg }}>
      <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}>
        <Image source={r.hero} style={styles.hero} />
        <View style={styles.sheet}>
          <View style={styles.titleRow}>
            <Text style={styles.name}>{r.name}</Text>
            <Chip label={r.isOpen ? 'open' : 'closed'} tone={r.isOpen ? 'open' : 'red'} />
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.cuisine}>{r.cuisine}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.rating}>
            <Text style={{ color: colors.gold }}>★ </Text>
            {r.rating.toFixed(1)}  <Text style={{ color: colors.muted }}>({r.reviews} reviews)</Text>
          </Text>
          <View style={styles.infoRow}>
            <Text style={styles.info}>📍 {r.distanceKm.toFixed(1)} km</Text>
            <View style={styles.vsep} />
            <Text style={styles.info}>
              <Feather name="clock" size={11} color={colors.textSoft} /> {r.deliveryMin} min
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.vsep} />
            <Text style={styles.info}>🛵 Free delivery above ₹{r.freeAbove}</Text>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.stats}>
            <Stat icon={<Feather name="shopping-bag" size={22} color={colors.green} />} top={`${r.offerPct}% OFF`} bottom={`Up to ₹${r.offerUpTo}`} bold />
            <View style={styles.statSep} />
            <Stat icon={<Feather name="star" size={22} color={colors.cyan} />} top={r.rating.toFixed(1)} bottom="Ratings" />
            <View style={styles.statSep} />
            <Stat icon={<MaterialCommunityIcons name="wallet" size={23} color="#2F7BFF" />} top="₹₹" bottom="Cost for two" />
            <View style={styles.statSep} />
            <Stat icon={<MaterialCommunityIcons name="alarm" size={23} color="#A855F7" />} top={r.etaRange} bottom="Delivery time" bold />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.menuHead}>
            <Text style={styles.menuTitle}>Popular Near You</Text>
            <Pressable hitSlop={8} onPress={() => toast('Full menu is shown below')}>
              <Text style={styles.fullMenu}>View Full Menu ›</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>

          {menu.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState icon="coffee" title="Menu coming soon" subtitle={`${r.name} hasn't published its menu yet.`} />
          ) : (
            menu.map((item) => {
              const qty = lines[item.id] ?? 0;
              return (
                <View key={item.id} style={styles.item}>
                  <Image source={item.image} style={styles.itemImg} />
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 12 }}>
                    <Text style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.itemDesc} numberOfLines={2}>{item.description}</Text>
                    <View style={styles.itemBottom}>
                      <Text style={styles.price}>₹{item.price}</Text>
                      {qty > 0 ? (
                        <QuantityStepper qty={qty} width={96} onMinus={() => decrement(item.id)} onPlus={() => increment(item.id)} />
                      ) : (
                        <AddButton onPress={() => onAdd(item)} />
                      )}
                    </View>
                  </View>
                </View>
              );
            })
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>

      {/* floating hero buttons */}
      <View style={[styles.heroBtns, { top: insets.top + 8 }]} pointerEvents="box-none">
        <Pressable style={styles.circle} onPress={() => (router.canGoBack() ? router.back() : router.replace('/(tabs)'))}>
          <Feather name="arrow-left" size={22} color="#fff" />
        </Pressable>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
          <Pressable style={styles.circle} onPress={() => toggleFav(r.id)}>
            <Feather name="heart" size={21} color={fav ? '#FF4D6D' : '#fff'} />
          </Pressable>
          <Pressable style={styles.circle} onPress={() => toast('More options are coming soon')}>
            <Feather name="more-vertical" size={21} color="#fff" />
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </View>

      <CartBar style={{ paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 12) }} />
    </View>
  );
}

function menuOwnerName(restaurantId: string) {
  const first = Object.keys(useCart.getState().lines)[0];
  const item = first ? getMenuItem(first) : undefined;
  return item ? restaurantId.split('-').map((w) => w[0].toUpperCase() + w.slice(1)).join(' ') : 'another restaurant';
}

function Stat({ icon, top, bottom, bold }: { icon: React.ReactNode; top: string; bottom: string; bold?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center' }}>
      {icon}
      <Text style={[styles.statTop, bold && { fontFamily: fonts.semi }]}>{top}</Text>
      <Text style={styles.statBottom}>{bottom}</Text>
    </View>
  );
}

  infoRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' },
  info: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 12.5, color: colors.textSoft },
  vsep: { width: 1, height: 14, backgroundColor: '#3A4558', marginHorizontal: 12 },
  stats: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 20, borderRadius: 18, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.cardBorder, backgroundColor: colors.card, paddingVertical: 14, paddingHorizontal: 6 },
  statSep: { width: 3, height: 34, borderRadius: 2, backgroundColor: '#3A4558' },
  statTop: { fontFamily: fonts.medium, fontSize: 12, color: '#fff', marginTop: 3 },
  statBottom: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 11.5, color: colors.muted },

});

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { View, Image, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity, ActivityIndicator, Modal, Alert } from 'react-native';
import MapView, { Marker, Polyline } from 'react-native-maps';
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { C } from './theme';
import { HOME, RESTAURANTS, byId, FEES } from './data';
import { useStore } from './store';
import { T, Card, Btn, Screen, Header, Pill, Row, Input, Stepper, RestHead } from './ui';

const logo = require('../assets/img/logo.png');
const rs = n => '₹' + n;
const pin = r => (
  <View style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}>
    <View style={{ backgroundColor: r.color, borderRadius: 10, padding: 6 }}><Feather name={r.icon} size={18} color="#fff" /></View>
    <View style={{ backgroundColor: '#fff', borderRadius: 6, paddingHorizontal: 5, marginTop: 2 }}><T z={9} f="s" c={r.color}>{r.min} min</T><T z={9} c="#222">{r.rating} ★</T></View>
  </View>
);

function Splash({ nav }) {
  const { user } = useStore();
  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', backgroundColor: C.bg }}>
      <Image source={logo} style={{ width: 280, height: 280 }} resizeMode="contain" />
      <Btn label="Get Started" s={{ width: 200, marginTop: 30 }} onPress={() => nav.reset(user ? 'Home' : 'Onboarding')} />
    </View>
  );
}
const OB = [
  ['Discover Food Around You', 'Explore nearby restaurants on an interactive map and find your next favourite meal.', 'map'],
  ['Choose What Fits You', 'Compare ratings, distance, delivery time, and food options to find the perfect place for you.', 'star'],
  ['Track Your Order Live', 'Follow your order in real time from the restaurant to your doorstep.', 'navigation'],
];
function Onboarding({ nav }) {
  const [i, setI] = useState(0);
  const [t, d, ic] = OB[i];
  return (
    <Screen>
      <TouchableOpacity style={{ alignSelf: 'flex-end' }} onPress={() => nav.reset('Signup')}><T c={C.mut}>skip</T></TouchableOpacity>
      <T f="b" z={22} s={{ textAlign: 'center', marginVertical: 14 }}>{t}</T>
      <Card s={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}><Feather name={ic} size={110} color={C.pri} /></Card>
      <T c={C.mut} s={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>{d}</T>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'center', marginVertical: 16 }}>
        {OB.map((_, k) => <View key={k} style={{ width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: 4, marginHorizontal: 3, backgroundColor: k === i ? C.pri : C.line }} />)}
      </View>
      <Btn label={i < 2 ? 'Next →' : 'Get Started →'} onPress={() => (i < 2 ? setI(i + 1) : nav.reset('Signup'))} />
    </Screen>
  );
}
function Signup({ nav }) {
  const { login } = useStore();
  const [f, setF] = useState({ name: '', email: '', pw: '', pw2: '' });
  const set = k => v => setF({ ...f, [k]: v });
  const go = () => {
    if (!f.name || !f.email || f.pw.length < 4) return Alert.alert('Check details', 'Enter name, email and a password (4+ characters).');
    if (f.pw !== f.pw2) return Alert.alert('Passwords differ', 'Confirm password must match.');
    login({ name: f.name, email: f.email }); nav.reset('Location');
  };
  return (
    <Screen>
      <Image source={logo} style={{ width: 90, height: 90, alignSelf: 'center' }} resizeMode="contain" />
      <T f="b" z={26} s={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Create Your Account</T>
      <T c={C.mut} s={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 18 }}>Join Navi Meal and discover food around you.</T>
      <Input icon="user" placeholder="Enter your name" value={f.name} onChangeText={set('name')} />
      <Input icon="mail" placeholder="Enter your email" autoCapitalize="none" value={f.email} onChangeText={set('email')} />
      <Input icon="lock" placeholder="Create a password" secureTextEntry value={f.pw} onChangeText={set('pw')} />
      <Input icon="lock" placeholder="Confirm password" secureTextEntry value={f.pw2} onChangeText={set('pw2')} />
      <Btn label="Create Account" onPress={go} s={{ marginTop: 6 }} />
      <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => nav.go('Login')} style={{ marginTop: 14 }}><T c={C.mut} s={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Already have an account? <T c={C.pri}>Login</T></T></TouchableOpacity>
    </Screen>
  );
}
function Login({ nav }) {
  const { login } = useStore();
  const [e, setE] = useState(''); const [p, setP] = useState('');
  return (
    <Screen>
      <Image source={logo} style={{ width: 90, height: 90, alignSelf: 'center' }} resizeMode="contain" />
      <T f="b" z={26} s={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Welcome Back 👋</T>
      <T c={C.mut} s={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 18 }}>Log in to continue your food journey.</T>
      <Input icon="mail" placeholder="Enter your email" autoCapitalize="none" value={e} onChangeText={setE} />
      <Input icon="lock" placeholder="Enter password" secureTextEntry value={p} onChangeText={setP} />
      <T c={C.pri} z={12} s={{ alignSelf: 'flex-end', marginBottom: 14 }}>Forgot Password?</T>
      <Btn label="Log In" onPress={() => { if (!e || !p) return Alert.alert('Enter email and password'); login({ name: e.split('@')[0], email: e }); nav.reset('Location'); }} />
      <T c={C.mut} s={{ textAlign: 'center', marginVertical: 14 }}>OR</T>
      <Btn ghost label="Continue with Google" onPress={() => Alert.alert('Google sign-in', 'Not enabled in this demo build.')} />
      <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => nav.back()} style={{ marginTop: 14 }}><T c={C.mut} s={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Don't have an account? <T c={C.pri}>Sign Up</T></T></TouchableOpacity>
    </Screen>
  );
}
function LocationScr({ nav }) {
  const ask = async () => { try { await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync(); } catch (e) {} nav.reset('Home'); };
  return (
    <Screen>
      <View style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        <Card s={{ width: 190, height: 190, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}><Feather name="map-pin" size={90} color={C.pri} /></Card>
        <T f="b" z={22} s={{ marginTop: 24 }}>Find Food Near You</T>
        <T c={C.mut} s={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 8 }}>Allow location access to discover nearby restaurants, get accurate delivery times, and track your order in real time.</T>
      </View>
      <Btn label="Allow Location" onPress={ask} />
      <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => nav.reset('Home')} style={{ marginTop: 14 }}><T c={C.mut} s={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Not Now</T></TouchableOpacity>
    </Screen>
  );
}
function Home({ nav }) {
  const [me, setMe] = useState(HOME);
  const [sel, setSel] = useState(null);
  useEffect(() => { (async () => { try { const { status } = await Location.getForegroundPermissionsAsync(); if (status === 'granted') { const l = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({}); setMe(l.coords); } } catch (e) {} })(); }, []);
  const region = { ...me, latitudeDelta: 0.03, longitudeDelta: 0.03 };
  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: C.bg }}>
      <MapView style={{ flex: 1 }} initialRegion={region} showsUserLocation>
        {RESTAURANTS.map(r => <Marker key={r.id} coordinate={r.loc} onPress={() => setSel(r.id)}>{pin(r)}</Marker>)}
      </MapView>
      <View style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, backgroundColor: C.bg, paddingTop: 50, paddingHorizontal: 20, paddingBottom: 12 }}>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 10 }}>
          <Feather name="map-pin" size={22} color={C.pri} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 8 }}><T z={11} c={C.mut}>Deliver to</T><T f="s">Anna Nagar, Chennai ⌄</T></View>
          <Feather name="bell" size={22} color="#fff" style={{ marginRight: 16 }} /><Feather name="user" size={22} color="#fff" />
        </View>
        <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => nav.reset('Explore')}><Card s={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: 12 }}><Feather name="search" size={18} color={C.mut} /><T c={C.mut} s={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 10 }}>Search restaurants or food</T><Feather name="filter" size={18} color={C.mut} /></Card></TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
      <View style={{ position: 'absolute', bottom: 86, left: 0, right: 0, backgroundColor: C.bg, paddingTop: 12, paddingBottom: 8 }}>
        <T f="s" z={16} s={{ marginHorizontal: 20, marginBottom: 8 }}>Nearby Restaurants</T>
        <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 20 }}>
          {RESTAURANTS.map(r => (
            <TouchableOpacity key={r.id} onPress={() => nav.go('Restaurant', { id: r.id })} style={{ width: 150, marginRight: 12, borderRadius: 14, overflow: 'hidden', borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: sel === r.id ? C.pri : C.line, backgroundColor: C.card }}>
              <Image source={r.img} style={{ width: '100%', height: 84 }} />
              <View style={{ padding: 8 }}><T f="s" z={13}>{r.name}</T><T z={10} c={C.mut}>{r.cuisine}</T><T z={10} c={C.mut}><T c="#FBBF24" z={10}>★ {r.rating}</T> {r.min} min • {r.km} km</T></View>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}
function Explore({ nav }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const list = RESTAURANTS.filter(r => (r.name + r.cuisine + r.menu.map(m => m.name).join()).toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()));
  return (
    <Screen bar>
      <Input icon="search" placeholder="Find foods or restaurants..." value={q} onChangeText={setQ} />
      <T f="s" z={18} s={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>Popular Restaurants</T>
      <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        {list.length === 0 && <T c={C.mut}>No restaurants match your search.</T>}
        {list.map(r => (
          <TouchableOpacity key={r.id} onPress={() => nav.go('Restaurant', { id: r.id })}>
            <Card s={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 12, padding: 10 }}>
              <Image source={r.img} style={{ width: 84, height: 84, borderRadius: 12, marginRight: 12 }} />
              <View><T f="s" z={16}>{r.name}</T><T c={C.mut} z={12}>{r.cuisine}</T><T z={12} c="#FBBF24">★ {r.rating} <T z={12} c={C.mut}>• {r.km} km • {r.min} mins</T></T><T z={12} c={C.mut}>{rs(r.cost)} for two</T></View>
            </Card>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
    </Screen>
  );
}

                  <T f="s" c={C.pri} z={16}>{rs(i.price)}</T>
                  <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => st.add(r.id, i.id)} style={{ borderColor: C.pri, borderWidth: 1.5, borderRadius: 10, paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 2, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    {mine && st.qty[i.id] ? <T c={C.pri} f="s">{st.qty[i.id]} </T> : null}<Feather name="plus" size={18} color={C.pri} /></TouchableOpacity>

        <Card s={{ flex: 1, marginRight: 10, paddingVertical: 12 }}><T z={13}>{st.count} items  {rs(st.sub)}</T></Card>

const Addr = ({ nav }) => (
  <Card s={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
