function Cart({ nav }) {
  const st = useStore();
  if (!st.count) return (<Screen bar><Header title="Your Cart" back={nav.back} /><T c={C.mut}>Your cart is empty. Add something tasty from a restaurant.</T></Screen>);
  return (
    <Screen bar>
      <Header title="Your Cart" back={nav.back} />
      <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        <RestHead r={st.rest} />
        {st.lines.map(l => (
          <Card key={l.id} s={{ flexDirection: 'row', marginBottom: 10, padding: 10 }}>
            <Image source={l.img} style={{ width: 100, height: 100, borderRadius: 14, marginRight: 14 }} />
            <View style={{ justifyContent: 'space-between' }}><T f="s" z={17}>{l.name}</T><T f="s" c={C.pri} z={17}>{rs(l.price)}</T><Stepper n={l.qty} inc={() => st.add(st.rest.id, l.id)} dec={() => st.dec(l.id)} /></View>
          </Card>
        ))}
        <Addr />
        <Card s={{ marginBottom: 12 }}><T f="s" z={17} s={{ marginBottom: 6 }}>Bill Details</T>
          <Row a="Item Total" b={rs(st.sub)} /><Row a="Delivery Fee" b={rs(FEES.delivery)} /><Row a="Discount" b={'−' + rs(FEES.discount)} cb={C.ok} /><Row a="Taxes & charges" b={rs(FEES.tax)} /><Row bold a="Total Amount" b={rs(st.total)} /></Card>
        <Btn label="Proceed to Checkout" onPress={() => nav.go('Checkout')} />
      </ScrollView>
    </Screen>
  );
}
function Checkout({ nav }) {
  const st = useStore();
  return (
    <Screen bar>
      <Header title="Checkout" back={nav.back} />
      <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        <Addr />
        <Card s={{ marginBottom: 12 }}><RestHead r={st.rest || RESTAURANTS[0]} /><T f="s" z={17}>Order Items</T>
          {st.lines.map(l => <Row key={l.id} a={`${l.qty} × ${l.name}`} b={rs(l.qty * l.price)} />)}

        <Card s={{ marginBottom: 12 }}><T f="s" z={17}>Payment Summary</T>
          <Row a="Item Total" b={rs(st.sub)} /><Row a="Delivery Fee" b={rs(FEES.delivery)} /><Row a="Taxes & charges" b={rs(FEES.tax)} /><Row a="Discount" b={'−' + rs(FEES.discount)} cb={C.ok} /><Row bold a="Total" b={rs(st.total)} /></Card>
        <Card s={{ marginBottom: 12 }}><Row a="Estimated delivery" b="10–15 min" /></Card>
        <Btn label="Continue to Payment" onPress={() => nav.go('Payment')} />
      </ScrollView>
    </Screen>
  );
}
const METHODS = [['UPI', 'Google Pay, PhonePe, Paytm', 'smartphone'], ['Credit / Debit Card', 'Visa, Mastercard, RuPay', 'credit-card'], ['Cash on Delivery', 'Pay when your order arrives', 'dollar-sign']];
function Payment({ nav }) {
  const st = useStore(); const [m, setM] = useState(0); const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const pay = () => { setBusy(true); setTimeout(() => { const o = st.place(METHODS[m][0]); nav.reset('Confirmed', { o }); }, 2200); };
  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="Payment" back={nav.back} />
      <T f="s" z={18} s={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>Choose payment method</T>
      {METHODS.map(([a, b, ic], k) => (
        <TouchableOpacity key={a} onPress={() => setM(k)}>
          <Card s={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 12, borderColor: m === k ? C.pri : C.line, borderWidth: m === k ? 2 : 1 }}>
            <Feather name={ic} size={22} color={C.pri} /><View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 14 }}><T f="s" z={16}>{a}</T><T z={12} c={C.mut}>{b}</T></View>
            <View style={{ width: 24, height: 24, borderRadius: 12, borderWidth: 2, borderColor: m === k ? C.pri : C.mut, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>{m === k && <View style={{ width: 12, height: 12, borderRadius: 6, backgroundColor: C.pri }} />}</View>
          </Card>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      ))}
      <Card><Row a="Order total" b={rs(st.total)} /><Row bold a="Amount to pay" b={rs(st.total)} /></Card>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', marginTop: 12 }}><Feather name="shield" size={18} color={C.ok} /><T c={C.mut} z={13} s={{ marginLeft: 8 }}>Payments are secure and encrypted</T></View>
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }} /><Btn label={`Pay Now ${rs(st.total)}`} onPress={pay} />
      <Modal visible={busy} transparent animationType="fade"><View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#050A14EE', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}><ActivityIndicator size="large" color={C.pri} /><T f="s" z={18} s={{ marginTop: 16 }}>Processing Payment...</T></View></Modal>
    </Screen>
  );
}
function Confirmed({ nav, p }) {
  const o = p.o; const r = byId(o.rid);
  return (
    <Screen>
      <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 40 }}>
        <View style={{ width: 170, height: 170, borderRadius: 85, backgroundColor: C.okbg, borderColor: C.ok, borderWidth: 3, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}><View style={{ width: 80, height: 80, borderRadius: 40, borderColor: C.ok, borderWidth: 2, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}><Feather name="check" size={40} color={C.ok} /></View></View>
        <T f="b" z={28} s={{ marginVertical: 14 }}>Order Confirmed!</T>
        <T c="#C9D3E3" s={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Your delicious order is being prepared by {r.name}.</T>
        <View style={{ backgroundColor: C.card, borderRadius: 16, paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingVertical: 6, marginVertical: 14 }}><T c={C.pri}>Order #{o.id}</T></View>
      </View>
      <Card s={{ marginBottom: 12 }}><T c={C.mut}>Estimated delivery time</T><T f="b" z={28}>10–15 min</T><T c={C.ok} z={13}>Restaurant is preparing your order</T></Card>
      <Card><RestHead r={r} /><Row a={`${o.items} items`} b={rs(o.sub)} /><Row a="Delivery + taxes" b={rs(FEES.delivery + FEES.tax)} /><Row a="Discount" b={'−' + rs(FEES.discount)} cb={C.ok} /><Row bold a={`Total paid via ${o.method === 'UPI' ? 'UPI' : o.method}`} b={rs(o.total)} /></Card>
      <View style={{ flex: 1 }} /><Btn label="Track Order" onPress={() => nav.reset('Track')} />
      <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => nav.reset('Home')} style={{ marginTop: 14 }}><T c={C.pri} f="m" s={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Back to Home</T></TouchableOpacity>
    </Screen>
  );
}
const N = 60;
function Track({ nav }) {
  const st = useStore(); const o = st.active;
  const [i, setI] = useState(0); const [stars, setStars] = useState(5); const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const r = o ? byId(o.rid) : RESTAURANTS[1];
  const pts = useRef(Array.from({ length: N + 1 }, (_, k) => { const t = k / N; return { latitude: r.loc.latitude + (HOME.latitude - r.loc.latitude) * t + 0.003 * Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2), longitude: r.loc.longitude + (HOME.longitude - r.loc.longitude) * t }; })).current;
  useEffect(() => { const id = setInterval(() => setI(v => { if (v >= N) { clearInterval(id); return v; } return v + 1; }), 500); return () => clearInterval(id); }, []);
  useEffect(() => { if (i >= N) setTimeout(() => setDone(true), 600); }, [i]);
  const mins = Math.max(1, Math.round((N - i) / 5));
  const on = i >= 8; const fin = i >= N;
  const tl = [[true, 'Order confirmed', '9:32'], [on, `Picked up from ${r.name}`, '9:41'], [on, 'Rider is on the way', fin ? '' : 'Now'], [fin, 'Delivered to Anna Nagar', fin ? 'Now' : '9:58']];
  const finish = () => { st.complete(); nav.reset('Orders'); };
  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="Track Order" back={() => nav.reset('Home')} />
      <View style={{ height: 300, borderRadius: 22, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        <MapView style={{ flex: 1 }} initialRegion={{ latitude: (r.loc.latitude + HOME.latitude) / 2, longitude: (r.loc.longitude + HOME.longitude) / 2, latitudeDelta: 0.025, longitudeDelta: 0.025 }}>
          <Polyline coordinates={pts} strokeColor="#4FC3F7" strokeWidth={5} />
          <Marker coordinate={r.loc}>{pin(r)}</Marker>
          <Marker coordinate={HOME}><View style={{ backgroundColor: '#F4623A', borderRadius: 24, padding: 10 }}><Feather name="home" size={20} color="#fff" /></View></Marker>
          <Marker coordinate={pts[Math.min(i, N)]} anchor={{ x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }}><View style={{ backgroundColor: C.pri, borderRadius: 26, padding: 10, borderColor: '#fff', borderWidth: 3 }}><Feather name="truck" size={22} color="#04101C" /></View></Marker>
        </MapView>
        <View style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 12, left: 12, backgroundColor: '#1B2433', borderRadius: 16, paddingHorizontal: 14, paddingVertical: 8 }}><T f="s" z={14}>{fin ? 'Delivered' : `Arriving in ${mins} min`}</T></View>
      </View>
      <Card s={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}><View><T c={C.mut} z={13}>Estimated arrival</T><T f="b" z={24}>9:58 PM</T></View><Pill label="● Live" /></View>
        {tl.map(([d, a, b], k) => <View key={k} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 12 }}><View style={{ width: 14, height: 14, borderRadius: 7, backgroundColor: d ? (k === 2 && !fin ? C.pri : C.ok) : C.line, marginRight: 14 }} /><T f={k === 2 && on && !fin ? 's' : 'r'} c={d ? C.txt : C.mut} s={{ flex: 1 }}>{a}</T><T c={C.mut} z={12}>{b}</T></View>)}
      </Card>
      <Card s={{ marginTop: 10, flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}><T c={C.mut}>{r.name} • {o ? o.items : 2} items</T><T f="s">{rs(o ? o.total : 480)}</T></Card>
      <Modal visible={done} transparent animationType="slide"><View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#000B', justifyContent: 'center', padding: 24 }}>
        <Card s={{ alignItems: 'center', padding: 24 }}>
          <T f="b" z={22}>Delivered! Enjoy your meal</T><T c={C.mut} s={{ marginVertical: 8 }}>How was your experience with NAVI MEAL?</T>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', marginVertical: 12 }}>{[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(k => <TouchableOpacity key={k} onPress={() => setStars(k)}><T z={38} c={k <= stars ? '#FBBF24' : C.line}>★</T></TouchableOpacity>)}</View>
          <Btn label="Submit Review" s={{ alignSelf: 'stretch' }} onPress={finish} />
        </Card>
      </View></Modal>
    </Screen>
  );
}

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\store.js ====================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { FEES, byId } from './data';

const Ctx = createContext(null);
export const useStore = () => useContext(Ctx);
const PAST = [
  { id: 'NM40111', rid: 'pizza', items: 3, total: 448, date: 'Sep 18, 2026', status: 'Delivered' },
  { id: 'NM40092', rid: 'brew', items: 1, total: 120, date: 'Sep 15, 2026', status: 'Cancelled' },
];
export function Provider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [rid, setRid] = useState(null);
  const [qty, setQty] = useState({});
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState(PAST);
  const [active, setActive] = useState(null);
  useEffect(() => { AsyncStorage.getItem('user').then(u => u && setUser(JSON.parse(u))).catch(() => {}); }, []);
  const login = u => { setUser(u); AsyncStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(u)).catch(() => {}); };
  const logout = () => { setUser(null); AsyncStorage.removeItem('user').catch(() => {}); };
  const add = (r, id) => { if (rid && rid !== r) setQty({}); setRid(r); setQty(q => ({ ...(rid && rid !== r ? {} : q), [id]: (rid && rid !== r ? 0 : q[id] || 0) + 1 })); };
  const dec = id => setQty(q => { const n = { ...q, [id]: (q[id] || 0) - 1 }; if (n[id] <= 0) delete n[id]; return n; });
  const rest = rid ? byId(rid) : null;
  const lines = rest ? rest.menu.filter(i => qty[i.id]).map(i => ({ ...i, qty: qty[i.id] })) : [];
  const count = lines.reduce((a, l) => a + l.qty, 0);
  const sub = lines.reduce((a, l) => a + l.qty * l.price, 0);
  const total = count ? sub + FEES.delivery + FEES.tax - FEES.discount : 0;
  const place = method => {
    const o = { id: 'NM' + Math.floor(10000 + Math.random() * 89999), rid, items: count, sub, total, method, lines, date: new Date().toDateString(), status: 'On the way' };
    setActive(o); setQty({}); setRid(null); return o;
  };
  const complete = () => { setOrders(os => [{ ...active, status: 'Delivered' }, ...os]); setActive(null); };
  return <Ctx.Provider value={{ user, login, logout, rest, lines, count, sub, total, add, dec, qty, place, active, complete, orders }}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\types.ts ====================

export type LatLng = { latitude: number; longitude: number };

export type Restaurant = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  cuisine: string; // e.g. "Italian • Pizza"
  tags: string[]; // category chips
  rating: number;
  reviews: string; // "1200+"
  deliveryMin: number;
  etaRange: string; // "20-25 min"
  distanceKm: number;
  bearing: number; // degrees from the customer (demo placement)
  priceForTwo: number;
  freeAbove: number;
  offerPct: number;
  offerUpTo: number;
  isOpen: boolean;
  hero: number; // require() id
  wide: number;
  logo: number | null;
  pinColor: string;
  pinIcon: string; // MaterialCommunityIcons name
};

export type MenuItem = {
  id: string;
  restaurantId: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  price: number;
  image: number;
  category: string;
  available: boolean;
};

export type OrderStatus =
  | 'PLACED'
  | 'CONFIRMED'
  | 'PREPARING'
  | 'READY_FOR_PICKUP'
  | 'PICKED_UP'
  | 'ON_THE_WAY'
  | 'DELIVERED'
  | 'CANCELLED';

export type PaymentMethod = 'UPI' | 'CARD' | 'COD';

export type OrderItem = { id: string; name: string; price: number; qty: number };

export type Order = {
  id: string; // NM48291
  restaurantId: string;
  restaurantName: string;
  restaurantCuisine: string;
  items: OrderItem[];
  itemCount: number;
  subtotal: number;
  deliveryFee: number;
  discount: number;
  taxes: number;
  total: number;
  paymentMethod: PaymentMethod;
  addressTitle: string;
  addressLine: string;
  status: OrderStatus;
  createdAt: number;
  timeline: Partial<Record<OrderStatus, number>>;
  simStartAt: number | null; // null => not a live (simulated) order
  origin: LatLng; // restaurant
  dest: LatLng; // customer
  riderStart: LatLng;
  route: [number, number][]; // restaurant -> customer polyline
  review?: { rating: number; comment: string };
  seeded?: boolean;
};

export type User = { id: string; name: string; email: string };

const TABS: { key: TabKey; label: string; icon: any; active: any }[] = [
  { key: 'index', label: 'Home', icon: 'home-outline', active: 'home' },
  { key: 'explore', label: 'Explore', icon: 'navigate-outline', active: 'navigate' },
  { key: 'orders', label: 'Orders', icon: 'bag-handle-outline', active: 'bag-handle' },
  { key: 'profile', label: 'Profile', icon: 'person-outline', active: 'person' },
];

export default function BottomNav({ active, onPress }: { active: TabKey; onPress: (k: TabKey) => void }) {

      {TABS.map((t) => {

            <Text style={[styles.label, { color: on ? colors.cyan : colors.textSoft }]}>{t.label}</Text>
          </Pressable>
        );
      })}
    </View>
  );
}

/** "2 items ₹448 | View Cart >" bar from the restaurant screen. Hidden while the cart is empty. */
export default function CartBar({ style }: { style?: object }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { count, subtotal } = useCartSummary();
  if (count === 0) return null;
  return (
    <View style={[styles.wrap, style]}>
      <Pressable style={styles.mini} onPress={() => router.push('/cart')}>
        <Feather name="shopping-cart" size={26} color={colors.cyan} />
        <View style={{ marginLeft: 12 }}>
          <Text style={styles.items}>{plural(count, 'item')}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.price}>{rupee(subtotal)}</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.viewSmall}>View Cart ›</Text>
      </Pressable>
      <Pressable style={styles.big} onPress={() => router.push('/cart')}>
        <Text style={styles.bigText}>View Cart ›</Text>
      </Pressable>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: { flexDirection: 'row', paddingHorizontal: 18, paddingVertical: 12, gap: 12, backgroundColor: colors.bgHome, borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: colors.navLine },

  viewSmall: { fontFamily: fonts.medium, fontSize: 11, color: colors.cyan, marginLeft: 'auto' },
  big: { flex: 1, height: 52, borderRadius: 16, backgroundColor: colors.cyan, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#2B6E90' },

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\components\Common.tsx ====================

import React from 'react';
import { Image, ImageSourcePropType, StyleProp, StyleSheet, Text, View, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { colors, fonts } from '../theme';
import { rupee } from '../utils/format';
import { Bill } from '../utils/bill';

export function Card({ children, style }: { children: React.ReactNode; style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle> }) {
  return <View style={[styles.card, style]}>{children}</View>;
}

export function Chip({ label, tone }: { label: string; tone: 'open' | 'green' | 'red' | 'blue' | 'live' }) {
  const map = {
    open: { bg: colors.greenBg, fg: colors.green },
    green: { bg: colors.greenBg, fg: colors.green },
    red: { bg: colors.redBg, fg: colors.red },
    blue: { bg: colors.blueBg, fg: colors.cyan },
    live: { bg: colors.greenBg, fg: colors.green },
  }[tone];
  return (
    <View style={[styles.chip, { backgroundColor: map.bg }]}>
      <Text style={[styles.chipText, { color: map.fg }]}>{label}</Text>
    </View>
  );
}

/** Logo tile + name + open chip + cuisine (cart / checkout / confirmed). */
export function RestaurantHeader({ logo, name, cuisine, open = true, size = 80 }: { logo: ImageSourcePropType | number | null; name: string; cuisine: string; open?: boolean; size?: number }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}>
      <View style={[styles.logoBox, { width: size, height: size, borderRadius: size * 0.22 }]}>
        {logo ? (
          <Image source={logo as any} style={{ width: size * 0.82, height: size * 0.82 }} resizeMode="contain" />
        ) : (
          <Feather name="coffee" size={size * 0.4} color="#555" />
        )}
      </View>
      <View style={{ marginLeft: 14, flex: 1 }}>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 10 }}>
          <Text style={[styles.rName, { fontSize: size > 70 ? 25 : 22 }]}>{name}</Text>
          {open ? <Chip label="open" tone="open" /> : <Chip label="closed" tone="red" />}
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.rCuisine}>{cuisine}</Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { LinearGradient } from 'expo-linear-gradient';

type Variant = 'splash' | 'card' | 'tab';
const CONFIG: Record<Variant, { colors: [string, string, ...string[]]; locations: [number, number, ...number[]] }> = {
  // blue glow top & bottom, black in the middle (splash / auth / onboarding)
  splash: { colors: ['#12307A', '#03081A', '#02060F', '#02060F', '#0E2A6E'], locations: [0, 0.27, 0.45, 0.68, 1] },
  card: { colors: ['#12307A', '#03081A', '#02060F', '#02060F', '#0E2A6E'], locations: [0, 0.27, 0.45, 0.68, 1] },
  // Orders / Explore / Profile: blue wash at the top fading to navy
  tab: { colors: ['#10307E', '#071636', '#040A18', '#030814'], locations: [0, 0.2, 0.45, 1] },
};

export default function GradientBg({ variant = 'splash' }: { variant?: Variant }) {
  const c = CONFIG[variant];
  return <LinearGradient colors={c.colors} locations={c.locations} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} />;
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { MaterialCommunityIcons, Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Marker } from 'react-native-maps';
import { LatLng } from '../types';
import { fonts } from '../theme';

/** react-native-maps custom markers need view tracking only for the first render. */
export function PinMarker({
  coordinate, children, onPress, anchor = { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }, zIndex,
}: {
  coordinate: LatLng; children: React.ReactNode; onPress?: () => void;
  anchor?: { x: number; y: number }; zIndex?: number;
}) {
  const [track, setTrack] = useState(true);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setTrack(false), 900);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);
  return (
    <Marker coordinate={coordinate} onPress={onPress} anchor={anchor} tracksViewChanges={track} zIndex={zIndex}>
      {children}
    </Marker>
  );
}

}

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\components\PrimaryButton.tsx ====================

import React from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Pressable, StyleProp, StyleSheet, Text, TextStyle, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { colors, fonts } from '../theme';

type Props = {
  title: string;
  onPress?: () => void;
  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
  textStyle?: StyleProp<TextStyle>;
  disabled?: boolean;
  loading?: boolean;
  height?: number;
  radius?: number;
  fontSize?: number;
};

export default function PrimaryButton({ title, onPress, style, textStyle, disabled, loading, height = 54, radius = 18, fontSize = 20 }: Props) {
  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={onPress}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      style={({ pressed }) => [styles.btn, { height, borderRadius: radius }, disabled && { opacity: 0.5 }, pressed && { opacity: 0.85 }, style]}
    >
      {loading ? (
        <ActivityIndicator color={colors.onCyan} />
      ) : (
        <Text style={[styles.text, { fontSize }, textStyle]}>{title}</Text>
      )}
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  btn: { backgroundColor: colors.cyan, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  text: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, color: colors.onCyan },
});

import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';

import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { colors, fonts } from '../theme';

export default function ScreenHeader({ title, onBack }: { title?: string; onBack?: () => void }) {

    <View style={[styles.row, { paddingTop: insets.top + 12 }]}>
      <Pressable hitSlop={14} onPress={back}>
        <Feather name="arrow-left" size={28} color={colors.cyan} />
      </Pressable>

    </View>
  );
}

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\components\SimulationRunner.tsx ====================

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { deliveryProvider } from '../services/delivery/SimulatedDeliveryProvider';
import { isActive, useOrders } from '../store/orders';

/**
 * Advances every live order's status from the delivery provider once per second.
 * The order store remains the single source of truth for screens (Orders, Track, ...).
 * When the real backend exists, this is replaced by a WebSocket/STOMP subscription.
 */
export default function SimulationRunner() {
  useEffect(() => {
    const tick = () => {
      const { orders, setStatus } = useOrders.getState();
      const now = Date.now();
      orders.forEach((o) => {
        if (o.simStartAt == null || !isActive(o)) return;
        const st = deliveryProvider.getStatus(o, now);
        if (st.status !== o.status) setStatus(o.id, st.status, now);
      });
    };
    tick();
    const id = setInterval(tick, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);
  return null;
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\components\TextField.tsx ====================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { KeyboardTypeOptions, Pressable, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { colors, fonts } from '../theme';

type Props = {
  label: string;
  icon: keyof typeof Feather.glyphMap;
  placeholder: string;
  value: string;
  onChangeText: (t: string) => void;
  secure?: boolean;
  keyboardType?: KeyboardTypeOptions;
  error?: string;
  autoCapitalize?: 'none' | 'sentences' | 'words';
};

export default function TextField({ label, icon, placeholder, value, onChangeText, secure, keyboardType, error, autoCapitalize = 'none' }: Props) {
  const [hidden, setHidden] = useState(!!secure);
  return (
    <View style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
      <Text style={styles.label}>{label}</Text>
      <View style={[styles.box, error ? { borderColor: colors.red } : null]}>
        <Feather name={icon} size={20} color="#B8C0CC" />
        <TextInput
          value={value}
          onChangeText={onChangeText}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          placeholderTextColor="#8E98A8"
          secureTextEntry={hidden}
          keyboardType={keyboardType}
          autoCapitalize={autoCapitalize}
          autoCorrect={false}
          style={styles.input}
        />
        {secure ? (
          <Pressable hitSlop={10} onPress={() => setHidden((h) => !h)}>
            <Feather name={hidden ? 'eye-off' : 'eye'} size={20} color="#B8C0CC" />
          </Pressable>
        ) : (
          <View style={{ width: 20 }} />
        )}
      </View>
      {error ? <Text style={styles.error}>{error}</Text> : null}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  label: { fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 14, color: colors.text, marginLeft: 14, marginBottom: 4 },
  box: {
    height: 52, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.inputBorder,
    flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 14, gap: 8,
  },
  input: { flex: 1, fontFamily: fonts.semi, fontSize: 14, color: colors.text, textAlign: 'center', paddingVertical: 0 },
  error: { fontFamily: fonts.regular, fontSize: 11.5, color: colors.red, marginLeft: 14, marginTop: 3 },
});



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\components\ToastHost.tsx ====================

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Animated, StyleSheet, Text } from 'react-native';
import { colors, fonts } from '../theme';
import { useUI } from '../store/ui';

export default function ToastHost() {
  const message = useUI((s) => s.message);
  const opacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const last = useRef('');
  if (message) last.current = message;
  useEffect(() => {
    Animated.timing(opacity, { toValue: message ? 1 : 0, duration: 180, useNativeDriver: true }).start();
  }, [message, opacity]);
  return (
    <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.wrap, { opacity }]}>
      <Text style={styles.text}>{last.current}</Text>
    </Animated.View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: {
    position: 'absolute',
    bottom: 120,
    alignSelf: 'center',
    backgroundColor: '#18263C',
    borderColor: colors.cardBorder,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 14,
    paddingHorizontal: 18,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    maxWidth: '86%',
  },
  text: { fontFamily: fonts.medium, color: colors.text, fontSize: 13, textAlign: 'center' },
});

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\hooks\useHydrated.ts ====================

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '../store/auth';
import { useCart } from '../store/cart';
import { useFavorites } from '../store/favorites';
import { useLocationStore } from '../store/location';
import { useOrders } from '../store/orders';

const stores = [useAuth, useCart, useOrders, useLocationStore, useFavorites];

/** true once every persisted store has been restored from AsyncStorage. */
export function useHydrated() {
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const check = () => setReady(stores.every((s) => s.persist.hasHydrated()));
    check();
    const unsubs = stores.map((s) => s.persist.onFinishHydration(check));
    return () => unsubs.forEach((u) => u());
  }, []);
  return ready;
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\hooks\useNow.ts ====================

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export function useNow(intervalMs = 1000, enabled = true) {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const id = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), intervalMs);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [intervalMs, enabled]);
  return now;
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\services\api.ts ====================

// "Backend" boundary. Today it reads local demo data; later replace the bodies with
// fetch() calls (e.g. GET /api/restaurants/nearby?lat=&lng=) without touching any screen.
import { RESTAURANTS } from '../data/restaurants';
import { getMenuFor } from '../data/menu';
import { LatLng, MenuItem, Restaurant } from '../types';
import { offsetPoint } from '../utils/geo';

export type RestaurantWithPos = Restaurant & { coords: LatLng };

export function nearbyRestaurants(anchor: LatLng): RestaurantWithPos[] {
  return RESTAURANTS.map((r) => ({ ...r, coords: offsetPoint(anchor, r.distanceKm, r.bearing) }));
}

export function restaurantById(id: string, anchor: LatLng): RestaurantWithPos | undefined {
  return nearbyRestaurants(anchor).find((r) => r.id === id);
}

export function menuForRestaurant(id: string): MenuItem[] {
  return getMenuFor(id);
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\services\auth.ts ====================

// Local mock auth (no backend yet). Passwords are SHA-256 hashed before being stored;
// the real app will call POST /api/auth/register & /login (Spring Security + JWT).
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import * as Crypto from 'expo-crypto';
import { User } from '../types';

const USERS_KEY = 'navimeal.users.v1';
type Stored = User & { passwordHash: string };

const hash = (pw: string, email: string) =>
  Crypto.digestStringAsync(Crypto.CryptoDigestAlgorithm.SHA256, `${email.toLowerCase()}::${pw}`);

async function readUsers(): Promise<Stored[]> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY);
    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as Stored[]) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export async function register(name: string, email: string, password: string): Promise<User> {
  const users = await readUsers();
  const e = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (users.some((u) => u.email === e)) throw new Error('An account with this email already exists.');
  const user: Stored = {
    id: `u_${Date.now()}`,
    name: name.trim(),
    email: e,
    passwordHash: await hash(password, e),
  };
  await AsyncStorage.setItem(USERS_KEY, JSON.stringify([...users, user]));
  return { id: user.id, name: user.name, email: user.email };
}

export async function login(email: string, password: string): Promise<User> {
  const users = await readUsers();
  const e = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  const found = users.find((u) => u.email === e);
  if (!found || found.passwordHash !== (await hash(password, e))) {
    throw new Error('Invalid email or password.');
  }
  return { id: found.id, name: found.name, email: found.email };
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\services\orders.ts ====================

// Order creation. In the real system this is POST /api/orders and the BACKEND
// recalculates prices from its own database. Here we recompute from the menu catalogue
// instead of trusting totals coming from the screens.
import { getMenuItem } from '../data/menu';
import { LatLng, Order, PaymentMethod } from '../types';
import { calcBill } from '../utils/bill';
import { offsetPoint } from '../utils/geo';
import { fetchRoute } from '../utils/route';
import { RestaurantWithPos } from './api';
import { deliveryProvider } from './delivery/SimulatedDeliveryProvider';

export type PlaceOrderInput = {
  restaurant: RestaurantWithPos;
  lines: Record<string, number>;
  method: PaymentMethod;
  dest: LatLng;
  addressTitle: string;
  addressLine: string;
};

export async function placeOrder(input: PlaceOrderInput): Promise<Order> {
  const items = Object.entries(input.lines)
    .map(([id, qty]) => ({ m: getMenuItem(id), qty }))
    .filter((l) => l.m && l.m.available && l.m.restaurantId === input.restaurant.id)
    .map((l) => ({ id: l.m!.id, name: l.m!.name, price: l.m!.price, qty: l.qty }));
  if (items.length === 0) throw new Error('Cart is empty');

  const subtotal = items.reduce((n, i) => n + i.price * i.qty, 0);
  const bill = calcBill(subtotal);
  const now = Date.now();
  const origin = input.restaurant.coords;
  const route = await fetchRoute(origin, input.dest);
  const riderStart = offsetPoint(origin, 0.55, (input.restaurant.bearing + 90) % 360);

  const order: Order = {
    id: `NM${10000 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 89999)}`,
    restaurantId: input.restaurant.id,
    restaurantName: input.restaurant.name,
    restaurantCuisine: input.restaurant.cuisine,
    items,
    itemCount: items.reduce((n, i) => n + i.qty, 0),
    ...bill,
    paymentMethod: input.method,
    addressTitle: input.addressTitle,
    addressLine: input.addressLine,
    status: 'CONFIRMED',
    createdAt: now,
    timeline: { PLACED: now, CONFIRMED: now },
    simStartAt: now,
    origin,
    dest: input.dest,
    riderStart,
    route,
  };
  await deliveryProvider.assignOrder(order);
  return order;
}

// MOCK payment. No real gateway is configured - this only simulates latency.
// A real provider (Razorpay, etc.) would be called from the backend, never from the app.
import { PaymentMethod } from '../types';

export type PaymentResult = { ok: true; reference: string } | { ok: false; reason: string };

export async function payMock(method: PaymentMethod, amount: number): Promise<PaymentResult> {
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2400));
  if (amount <= 0) return { ok: false, reason: 'Invalid amount' };
  return { ok: true, reference: `MOCK-${method}-${Date.now()}` };
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\services\delivery\DeliveryProvider.ts ====================

import { LatLng, Order, OrderStatus } from '../../types';

export type DeliveryState = {
  status: OrderStatus;
  /** 0..1 progress of the whole delivery */
  overall: number;
  /** 0..1 progress inside the current phase */
  phaseProgress: number;
  phase: 'toRestaurant' | 'pickup' | 'toCustomer' | 'done';
  etaMin: number;
  riderLocation: LatLng;
};

/**
 * Delivery provider abstraction. The order system only depends on this interface,
 * so the simulation can later be replaced by our own Partner app (WebSocket GPS)
 * or an external logistics API without touching screens.
 */
export interface DeliveryProvider {
  assignOrder(order: Order): Promise<{ deliveryId: string; riderName: string }>;
  getStatus(order: Order, now?: number): DeliveryState;
  getLocation(order: Order, now?: number): LatLng;
  cancelDelivery(order: Order): Promise<void>;
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\services\delivery\SimulatedDeliveryProvider.ts ====================

import { LatLng, Order, OrderStatus } from '../../types';
import { lerp } from '../../utils/geo';
import { pointAlong } from '../../utils/route';
import { DeliveryProvider, DeliveryState } from './DeliveryProvider';

/** Demo timings (seconds). Make these bigger for a slower, more realistic demo. */
export const DEMO = {
  confirmSec: 1.5,
  prepSec: 10, // rider rides to restaurant while food is prepared
  pickupSec: 3,
  rideSec: 45, // restaurant -> customer
  etaTotalMin: 15, // what the "Arriving in N min" label counts down from
};

const ease = (t: number) => t * t * (3 - 2 * t);

export class SimulatedDeliveryProvider implements DeliveryProvider {
  async assignOrder(order: Order) {
    return { deliveryId: `D-${order.id}`, riderName: 'Ravi K' };
  }

  async cancelDelivery(): Promise<void> {}

  getStatus(order: Order, now: number = Date.now()): DeliveryState {
    if (order.simStartAt == null) {
      return {
        status: order.status,
        overall: order.status === 'DELIVERED' ? 1 : 0,
        phaseProgress: 1,
        phase: 'done',
        etaMin: 0,
        riderLocation: order.dest,
      };
    }
    const t = Math.max(0, (now - order.simStartAt) / 1000);
    const { confirmSec, prepSec, pickupSec, rideSec, etaTotalMin } = DEMO;
    const total = prepSec + pickupSec + rideSec;

    let status: OrderStatus;
    let phase: DeliveryState['phase'];
    let pp: number;
    if (t < prepSec) {
      status = t < confirmSec ? 'CONFIRMED' : 'PREPARING';
      phase = 'toRestaurant';
      pp = t / prepSec;
    } else if (t < prepSec + pickupSec) {
      status = 'PICKED_UP';
      phase = 'pickup';
      pp = (t - prepSec) / pickupSec;
    } else if (t < total) {
      status = 'ON_THE_WAY';
      phase = 'toCustomer';
      pp = (t - prepSec - pickupSec) / rideSec;
    } else {
      status = 'DELIVERED';
      phase = 'done';
      pp = 1;
    }
    const overall = Math.min(1, t / total);
    const etaMin = status === 'DELIVERED' ? 0 : Math.max(1, Math.ceil(etaTotalMin * (1 - overall)));
    return { status, overall, phaseProgress: pp, phase, etaMin, riderLocation: this.locationFor(order, phase, pp) };
  }

  getLocation(order: Order, now: number = Date.now()): LatLng {
    return this.getStatus(order, now).riderLocation;
  }

  private locationFor(order: Order, phase: DeliveryState['phase'], pp: number): LatLng {
    switch (phase) {
      case 'toRestaurant':
        return lerp(order.riderStart, order.origin, ease(pp));
      case 'pickup':
        return order.origin;
      case 'toCustomer':
        return pointAlong(order.route, pp);
      default:
        return order.dest;
    }
  }
}

export const deliveryProvider: DeliveryProvider = new SimulatedDeliveryProvider();

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\store\cart.ts ====================

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { createJSONStorage, persist } from 'zustand/middleware';
import { getMenuItem } from '../data/menu';
import { MenuItem } from '../types';
import { calcBill } from '../utils/bill';

type CartState = {
  restaurantId: string | null;
  lines: Record<string, number>; // itemId -> qty
  /** returns false when the item belongs to another restaurant (caller should confirm replace) */
  add: (item: MenuItem) => boolean;
  replaceWith: (item: MenuItem) => void;
  increment: (id: string) => void;
  decrement: (id: string) => void;
  clear: () => void;
};

export const useCart = create<CartState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      restaurantId: null,
      lines: {},
      add: (item) => {
        const { restaurantId, lines } = get();
        const hasItems = Object.keys(lines).length > 0;
        if (hasItems && restaurantId && restaurantId !== item.restaurantId) return false;
        set({ restaurantId: item.restaurantId, lines: { ...lines, [item.id]: (lines[item.id] ?? 0) + 1 } });
        return true;
      },
      replaceWith: (item) => set({ restaurantId: item.restaurantId, lines: { [item.id]: 1 } }),
      increment: (id) => set((s) => ({ lines: { ...s.lines, [id]: (s.lines[id] ?? 0) + 1 } })),
      decrement: (id) =>
        set((s) => {
          const q = (s.lines[id] ?? 0) - 1;
          const lines = { ...s.lines };
          if (q <= 0) delete lines[id];
          else lines[id] = q;
          const empty = Object.keys(lines).length === 0;
          return { lines, restaurantId: empty ? null : s.restaurantId };
        }),
      clear: () => set({ restaurantId: null, lines: {} }),
    }),
    { name: 'navimeal.cart.v1', storage: createJSONStorage(() => AsyncStorage) }
  )
);

/** Derived cart data: items with menu info, counts and the bill. */
export function useCartSummary() {
  const lines = useCart((s) => s.lines);
  const restaurantId = useCart((s) => s.restaurantId);
  return useMemo(() => {
    const items = Object.entries(lines)
      .map(([id, qty]) => ({ item: getMenuItem(id), qty }))
      .filter((l): l is { item: MenuItem; qty: number } => !!l.item && l.item.available);
    const count = items.reduce((n, l) => n + l.qty, 0);
    const subtotal = items.reduce((n, l) => n + l.qty * l.item.price, 0);
    return { restaurantId, items, count, subtotal, bill: calcBill(subtotal) };
  }, [lines, restaurantId]);
}

==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\store\location.ts ====================

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { createJSONStorage, persist } from 'zustand/middleware';
import { FALLBACK_LOCATION, FALLBACK_PLACE } from '../theme';
import { LatLng } from '../types';

type LocState = {
  asked: boolean; // location permission screen already shown
  coords: LatLng | null; // real device location (null => fallback)
  placeName: string;
  requestPermission: () => Promise<boolean>;
  refresh: () => Promise<void>;
  markAsked: () => void;
};

export const useLocationStore = create<LocState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      asked: false,
      coords: null,
      placeName: FALLBACK_PLACE,
      markAsked: () => set({ asked: true }),
      requestPermission: async () => {
        try {
          const { status } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
          set({ asked: true });
          if (status === 'granted') {
            await get().refresh();
            return true;
          }
        } catch {}
        set({ asked: true, coords: null, placeName: FALLBACK_PLACE });
        return false;
      },
      refresh: async () => {
        try {
          const perm = await Location.getForegroundPermissionsAsync();
          if (perm.status !== 'granted') {
            set({ coords: null, placeName: FALLBACK_PLACE });
            return;
          }
          const pos = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({ accuracy: Location.Accuracy.Balanced });
          const coords = { latitude: pos.coords.latitude, longitude: pos.coords.longitude };
          let placeName = 'Current location';
          try {
            const [a] = await Location.reverseGeocodeAsync(coords);
            const area = a?.district || a?.subregion || a?.street || a?.name;
            const city = a?.city || a?.region;
            const joined = [area, city].filter(Boolean).join(', ');
            if (joined) placeName = joined;
          } catch {}
          set({ coords, placeName });
        } catch {
          set({ coords: null, placeName: FALLBACK_PLACE });
        }
      },
    }),
    {
      name: 'navimeal.location.v1',
      storage: createJSONStorage(() => AsyncStorage),
      partialize: (s) => ({ asked: s.asked }),
    }
  )
);

/** Anchor used for every map: real location when available, otherwise the Anna Nagar fallback. */
export const useAnchor = (): LatLng => {
  const coords = useLocationStore((s) => s.coords);
  return coords ?? FALLBACK_LOCATION;
};

    status: 'CANCELLED', createdAt: new Date(2026, 8, 15, 9, 5).getTime(), timeline: {},
    simStartAt: null, origin: zero, dest: zero, riderStart: zero, route: [], seeded: true,
  },
];

type OrdersState = {
  orders: Order[];
  addOrder: (o: Order) => void;
  setStatus: (id: string, status: OrderStatus, ts?: number) => void;
  setReview: (id: string, rating: number, comment: string) => void;
};

export const useOrders = create<OrdersState>()(
  persist(
    (set) => ({
      orders: SEED,
      addOrder: (o) => set((s) => ({ orders: [o, ...s.orders] })),
      setStatus: (id, status, ts = Date.now()) =>
        set((s) => ({
          orders: s.orders.map((o) =>
            o.id === id ? { ...o, status, timeline: { ...o.timeline, [status]: ts } } : o
          ),
        })),
      setReview: (id, rating, comment) =>
        set((s) => ({ orders: s.orders.map((o) => (o.id === id ? { ...o, review: { rating, comment } } : o)) })),
    }),
    { name: 'navimeal.orders.v1', storage: createJSONStorage(() => AsyncStorage) }
  )
);

export const isActive = (o: Order) => o.status !== 'DELIVERED' && o.status !== 'CANCELLED';

import { create } from 'zustand';

type UI = { message: string | null; show: (m: string) => void; hide: () => void };
let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;

export const useUI = create<UI>((set) => ({
  message: null,
  show: (message) => {
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    set({ message });
    timer = setTimeout(() => set({ message: null }), 2200);
  },
  hide: () => set({ message: null }),
}));

export const toast = (m: string) => useUI.getState().show(m);



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\utils\bill.ts ====================

// Bill rules. These mirror the Figma numbers (₹448 -> ₹30 fee, -₹20 promo, ₹22 taxes => ₹480).
// In a real product the BACKEND recalculates this - never trust totals from the client.
export const DELIVERY_FEE = 30;
export const TAXES_AND_CHARGES = 22;
export const PROMO_DISCOUNT = 20;
export const PROMO_MIN_SUBTOTAL = 200;

export type Bill = {
  subtotal: number;
  deliveryFee: number;
  discount: number;
  taxes: number;
  total: number;
};

export function calcBill(subtotal: number): Bill {
  if (subtotal <= 0) return { subtotal: 0, deliveryFee: 0, discount: 0, taxes: 0, total: 0 };
  const discount = subtotal >= PROMO_MIN_SUBTOTAL ? PROMO_DISCOUNT : 0;
  const total = subtotal + DELIVERY_FEE - discount + TAXES_AND_CHARGES;
  return { subtotal, deliveryFee: DELIVERY_FEE, discount, taxes: TAXES_AND_CHARGES, total };
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\utils\format.ts ====================

export const rupee = (n: number) => `₹${Math.round(n)}`;

export function formatClock(ms: number, withMeridiem = true): string {
  const d = new Date(ms);
  let h = d.getHours();
  const m = d.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0');
  const mer = h >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
  h = h % 12 || 12;
  return withMeridiem ? `${h}:${m} ${mer}` : `${h}:${m}`;
}

const MONTHS = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
export function formatDate(ms: number): string {
  const d = new Date(ms);
  return `${MONTHS[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getDate()}, ${d.getFullYear()}`;
}

export const plural = (n: number, w: string) => `${n} ${w}${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\utils\geo.ts ====================

import { LatLng } from '../types';

const R = 6371; // km
const rad = (d: number) => (d * Math.PI) / 180;
const deg = (r: number) => (r * 180) / Math.PI;

export function haversineKm(a: LatLng, b: LatLng): number {
  const dLat = rad(b.latitude - a.latitude);
  const dLng = rad(b.longitude - a.longitude);
  const s =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 +
    Math.cos(rad(a.latitude)) * Math.cos(rad(b.latitude)) * Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2;
  return 2 * R * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(s));
}

/** Point at `distKm` from `from`, travelling on `bearingDeg`. */
export function offsetPoint(from: LatLng, distKm: number, bearingDeg: number): LatLng {
  const br = rad(bearingDeg);
  const lat1 = rad(from.latitude);
  const lng1 = rad(from.longitude);
  const d = distKm / R;
  const lat2 = Math.asin(Math.sin(lat1) * Math.cos(d) + Math.cos(lat1) * Math.sin(d) * Math.cos(br));
  const lng2 =
    lng1 +
    Math.atan2(Math.sin(br) * Math.sin(d) * Math.cos(lat1), Math.cos(d) - Math.sin(lat1) * Math.sin(lat2));
  return { latitude: deg(lat2), longitude: deg(lng2) };
}

export function lerp(a: LatLng, b: LatLng, t: number): LatLng {
  return {
    latitude: a.latitude + (b.latitude - a.latitude) * t,
    longitude: a.longitude + (b.longitude - a.longitude) * t,
  };
}



==================== C:\Users\baran\Downloads\navimeal\navimeal\src\utils\route.ts ====================

import { LatLng } from '../types';
import { haversineKm } from './geo';

export type Route = [number, number][]; // [lat, lng]

/** Smooth S-curve between two points (used offline / when routing fails). */
export function fallbackRoute(a: LatLng, b: LatLng, n = 80): Route {
  const dx = b.longitude - a.longitude;
  const dy = b.latitude - a.latitude;
  const len = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
  const nx = -dy / len;
  const ny = dx / len;
  const amp = len * 0.14;
  const pts: Route = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    const t = i / n;
    const wob = Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2) * amp * Math.sin(t * Math.PI);
    pts.push([a.latitude + dy * t + ny * wob, a.longitude + dx * t + nx * wob]);
  }
  return pts;
}

/**
 * Road route from the free public OSRM demo server (no API key).
 * Falls back to a curved line on any failure / timeout.
 * NOTE: the demo server is for light testing only - use your own routing service in production.
 */
export async function fetchRoute(a: LatLng, b: LatLng, timeoutMs = 3500): Promise<Route> {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const url =
      `https://router.project-osrm.org/route/v1/driving/` +
      `${a.longitude},${a.latitude};${b.longitude},${b.latitude}?overview=full&geometries=geojson`;
    const res = await fetch(url, { signal: ctrl.signal });
    const json = await res.json();
    const coords: [number, number][] | undefined = json?.routes?.[0]?.geometry?.coordinates;
    if (!coords || coords.length < 2) return fallbackRoute(a, b);
    const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(coords.length / 140));
    const pts: Route = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < coords.length; i += step) pts.push([coords[i][1], coords[i][0]]);
    const last = coords[coords.length - 1];
    pts.push([last[1], last[0]]);
    return [[a.latitude, a.longitude], ...pts, [b.latitude, b.longitude]];
  } catch {
    return fallbackRoute(a, b);
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

export function pointAlong(route: Route, f: number): LatLng {
  if (route.length === 0) return { latitude: 0, longitude: 0 };
  if (route.length === 1 || f <= 0) return { latitude: route[0][0], longitude: route[0][1] };
  if (f >= 1) {
    const l = route[route.length - 1];
    return { latitude: l[0], longitude: l[1] };
  }
  const seg: number[] = [];
  let total = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i < route.length; i++) {
    const d = haversineKm(
      { latitude: route[i - 1][0], longitude: route[i - 1][1] },
      { latitude: route[i][0], longitude: route[i][1] }
    );
    seg.push(d);
    total += d;
  }
  let target = total * f;
  for (let i = 0; i < seg.length; i++) {
    if (target <= seg[i] || i === seg.length - 1) {
      const t = seg[i] === 0 ? 0 : Math.min(1, target / seg[i]);
      return {
        latitude: route[i][0] + (route[i + 1][0] - route[i][0]) * t,
        longitude: route[i][1] + (route[i + 1][1] - route[i][1]) * t,
      };
    }
    target -= seg[i];
  }
  const l = route[route.length - 1];
  return { latitude: l[0], longitude: l[1] };
}

export const toLatLng = (r: Route): LatLng[] => r.map(([latitude, longitude]) => ({ latitude, longitude }));
